#### REQ-FLOW-039 — The injection screen lets secret references through and names each line it refuses (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-screen-references`. The block-severity screen over owner text a write keeps next to
engine output — the merge lane (`AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md` and every supplemented file), the text Codex's
`AGENTS.override.md` repeats, and `.gitignore` — does not refuse a secret-shaped assignment whose whole value is one
reference: `${{ <dotted.name> }}`, `${NAME}`, `$NAME`, `process.env.NAME`, `process.env["NAME"]`, `os.environ["NAME"]`,
`os.environ.get("NAME")` or `os.getenv("NAME")`. A literal anywhere in the value still refuses, and every other rule is
unchanged. `.gitignore` comment lines are not screened. A refusal names each finding as `path:line:column`, the rule id
and a redacted excerpt (the key and the value's length), one finding per rule and line, with a repository-relative path
and the file's own line.

- **Units:** `u1-screen-references`.
- **Evidence (before):** measured 2026-10-06 on `d10db029` (r2 M5, M6): three documented reference lines refused `init`
  (exit 1) at offsets 79, 119 and 169 with every match redacted; one line after a `…` was reported at offsets 64 and 66;
  a `.gitignore` comment refused `init`. Rule `src/denyscan/denyScan.ts:739`; gates `src/merge/safeWrite.ts:326-376`,
  `src/mcp/env.ts:486-542`.
- **Proof:** `test/denyscan/denyScan.test.ts`, `test/merge/safeWrite.test.ts`, `test/mcp/env.test.ts`,
  `test/cli/commands/initApply.test.ts`; QA.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-039**):
- GIVEN an owner `AGENTS.md` holding `GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}`, `deploy-token: ${{ secrets.MY_TOKEN }}`
  and `api_key = os.environ["API_KEY"]` WHEN `init -y --import-config supplement` runs THEN it exits 0 and every owner
  byte is kept below the block.
- GIVEN the same file with `api_key: ` and twenty literal characters on line 9 WHEN `init` runs THEN it exits 1 and names
  `AGENTS.md:9:1 inline-secret-assignment api_key: [redacted 20 chars]`.
- GIVEN `token: ${{ secrets.X }} and hunter2hunter2` or `password = ${DB_PASSWORD:-hunter2secret}` THEN the line is
  refused.
- GIVEN a line a normalised copy also matches THEN it is reported once.
- GIVEN a `.gitignore` whose comment reads `# secret: files matching *.pem stay local` WHEN setup appends a line THEN it
  does so.
- GIVEN a supplemented file whose owner text below the block holds a refused line THEN the reported line is the file's.

#### REQ-FLOW-040 — A refused `init` writes nothing, and its dry run says so (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-screen-references`. Before it creates the state directory or writes any file, `init`
checks every refusal a write would raise: the screen (REQ-FLOW-039), a symbolic or hard link where it would keep or back
up bytes, a refused republished source, and a `.gitignore` it cannot write. Any refusal stops the run with exit 1 and
nothing written, and `init --dry-run` reports the same refusal with the same exit code. A file `init` cannot prove it
owns is not a refusal: it is left alone and reported skipped. `sync` stays per path.

- **Units:** `u1-screen-references`.
- **Evidence (before):** r2 M5, M6: a refused `init` left 66 files (`.claude/` 59, `.stamity/` 7), including a
  `.claude/settings.json` wiring five hook events, and no manifest; `check` said "not initialised" and `clean -y`
  "Nothing to clean"; `init --dry-run` exited 0 with `ok: true`. Order `src/cli/commands/init/apply.ts:226-241`,
  `:319-379`, `:426-433`; refusal-blind preview `:538-560`.
- **Proof:** `test/cli/commands/init.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/initApply.test.ts`; QA.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-040**):
- GIVEN an owner `AGENTS.md` with a refused line WHEN `init -y --tools claude,cursor --import-config supplement` runs
  THEN it exits 1 and `git status --porcelain --ignored` prints what it printed before.
- GIVEN the same WHEN `init -y --dry-run` runs THEN it exits 1 with the same findings and the tree is unchanged.
- GIVEN a hard-linked `AGENTS.md`, or a `.gitignore` that is a symbolic link and a setup that adds a line, WHEN
  `init -y` runs THEN it exits 1 and nothing is written.
- GIVEN a refused `init --json` THEN the document carries `refusals[].path`, `kind` and `findings[].line`.

#### REQ-FLOW-041 — Each instruction file has its own import mode, changed only through `config set` and `sync` (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-import-mode-per-file` (and `u1-init-force-charter` for `--force`). `--import-config`
takes a mode for every detected instruction file and, repeatably, `<path>=<mode>` for one of `AGENTS.md`, `AGENT.md`,
`CLAUDE.md` and `.github/copilot-instructions.md`. The keys `importChoice.<path>` read and change one recorded decision.
`config set` moves the owner's bytes itself: to `replace` it backs up the file's owner text (any managed block removed)
to `<path>.bak` or `<path>.bak.<8 hex>` beside it and records the backup; from `replace` it restores that backup over the
engine's unedited file. `sync` overwrites a `replace` path only when the target is the engine's own output or the
recorded backup holds exactly its owner text, and never takes or restores an import backup; `init --force` refuses a flag
that would change a recorded decision. `replace` replaces `CLAUDE.md` as it does `AGENTS.md`, behind the recorded backup,
and keeps nothing to screen. A decision that leaves Claude Code or Codex with no charter warns, naming the client and the
consequence. `init --strict` exits 1 when any planned path was skipped; the default stays 0.

- **Units:** `u1-import-mode-per-file`, `u1-init-force-charter`.
- **Evidence (before):** r2 M7: one mode for every file; `config get/set importChoice` → "unknown config key"; `replace`
  left `CLAUDE.md` supplemented and screened while the panel claimed a `.bak`; `skip` wrote no charter for Claude or
  Codex. r2 M4b: a committed `supplement` → `replace` flip made `sync` replace the owner's `AGENTS.md`. r2 M8: `init`
  exited 0 with a skipped path where `sync` exits 1. Code `src/cli/commands/init.ts:916-1011`, `:618-662`;
  `src/cli/commands/config.ts:786-795`; `src/cli/commands/init/apply.ts:298-300`.
- **Proof:** `test/cli/commands/init.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/config.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts`,
  `test/cli/commands/roundTrip.test.ts`, `test/cli/engine/importChoice.test.ts`; QA.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-041**):
- GIVEN `--import-config supplement --import-config CLAUDE.md=skip` THEN `AGENTS.md` is supplemented, `CLAUDE.md` is
  byte-identical, and a warning names Claude Code.
- GIVEN `--import-config CLAUDE.md=replace` THEN `CLAUDE.md.bak` holds the original, the decision records its sha256, and
  `CLAUDE.md` is the engine's.
- GIVEN an unknown mode or path, one path twice, or two bare modes THEN `init` exits 2 and writes nothing.
- GIVEN `config set importChoice.AGENTS.md replace` and then `sync` THEN `AGENTS.md.bak` is the owner's original byte for
  byte, `AGENTS.md` is the charter, and a later `clean -y` restores the original with no `.bak` left.
- GIVEN a replaced file edited after `sync` WHEN `config set importChoice.<path> supplement` runs THEN it exits 1 and
  nothing changes.
- GIVEN a hand edit flipping a decision to `replace` with no recorded backup WHEN `sync` runs THEN the owner's file is
  byte-identical and `sync` exits 1.
- GIVEN an unproven path WHEN `init --strict` runs THEN it exits 1 with every other file written; without `--strict` it
  exits 0.
- GIVEN a recorded `skip` WHEN `init --force --import-config <path>=supplement` runs THEN it exits 1 naming the `config
  set` command.

#### REQ-FLOW-042 — `check` tells a needed re-sync from tampering (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-drift-cause`. `check` classifies every drift row. `resync`: the plan differs while the
bytes the engine wrote at the path are intact (they hash to what the ledger recorded), so only the repository's own
inputs moved — detection inputs, owner text, the pinned version — or the path is a new output. `tamper`: an edited or missing engine file, a
collision, a pending deletion, or a row with no recorded hash. `check` exits 3 when no doctor row fails and every drift
row is `resync`, 1 for any other failure, 0 when green; exit 3 is never a pass, and no other verb returns it. `--json`
carries `drift.rows[]` with `cause` and `reason`, `drift.cause`, and for exit 3 `error.code: RESYNC_NEEDED` whose `next`
is the pinned `sync`.

- **Units:** `u1-drift-cause`.
- **Evidence (before):** r2 M11 (2026-10-06): a new `lint` script (17 files), a lockfile, a new owner workflow, a
  Dependabot bump in the owner's `ci.yml`, a new `.nvmrc`, and with Codex an owner edit outside `AGENTS.md`'s block each
  failed `check` alone; every drift exits 1 with `INTEGRITY_ERROR` (`src/cli/commands/check.ts:2130`, `:2197`); the exit
  contract was 0/1/2 (`src/cli/kit/program.ts:29-35`).
- **Proof:** `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`, `test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts`, `test/cli/kit.test.ts`; QA.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-042**):
- GIVEN a committed setup WHEN only a `package.json` script, a lockfile, a first workflow, a `.nvmrc`, or (with Codex) an
  owner line outside `AGENTS.md`'s block changes THEN `check` exits 3, `drift.cause` is `resync`, and `error.next` names
  the pinned `sync`.
- GIVEN that run followed by the pinned `sync` THEN `check` exits 0.
- GIVEN an edit inside a generated file THEN `check` exits 1 and that row reads `tamper/edited`, beside any `resync` rows.
- GIVEN a missing ledgered file, a collision, or a pending deletion THEN `check` exits 1.
- GIVEN a doctor row that fails THEN `check` exits 1 whatever the drift.
- GIVEN the CLI reference THEN it lists four exit statuses, 3 reading "check only: re-sync needed … Never a pass."

#### REQ-FLOW-043 — Detection reads the repository root, and its inputs are named (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, units `u1-drift-cause` and `u1-copilot-setup-steps`. Detection reads the repository root's own
files and entries, the names of the files in `.github/workflows/`, and the `package.json` of each monorepo package the
root's workspace configuration names; the Copilot setup workflow also reads `.nvmrc`, `.node-version` and
`engines.node`. It reads no other folder, so a pin folder such as `.tools/stamity/` changes no generated file unless the
root's workspace configuration names it. The owner's workflow `uses:` lines are not an input. The troubleshooting guide
lists the inputs beside the exit-3 explanation.

- **Units:** `u1-drift-cause`, `u1-copilot-setup-steps`.
- **Evidence (before):** the probes `src/detect/repoAnalyzer.ts:407-437`, workflows `:756-772`, workspace packages
  `:596-625`, `:905-930`; Copilot's pins `src/adapters/copilot.ts:685-760`; r2 M14: a committed
  `.tools/stamity/{package.json,package-lock.json,.npmrc}` left `check` clean on a Terraform and a Node base; nothing
  stated it (`docs/specs/everyday-flows.md:161` covers manifest reads only).
- **Proof:** `test/detect/repoAnalyzer.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts`, `test/docsPages.test.ts`.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-043**):
- GIVEN a Node fixture and a copy with `.tools/stamity/{package.json (a lint script), package-lock.json, .npmrc}`
  committed THEN detection summaries are equal and every planned output is byte-identical.
- GIVEN an owner workflow whose `actions/checkout` pin is bumped after `sync` THEN `check` reports no drift.
- GIVEN `docs/troubleshooting.md` THEN its exit-3 section lists the inputs above.

#### REQ-FLOW-044 — Setup says when git ignores a file it writes, and `check` fails on it (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-ignored-output`. `init` and `sync` ask git which planned paths it ignores and warn once
per rule, naming the rule's file, line and pattern and the paths it hides; they still write the files. `check`'s
`ignored-output` row fails naming each ledgered path, or the manifest, that git ignores and the rule that does it, and
its next step is the `.gitignore` edit, not `sync`. With no git the row reads `skipped` with its reason and setup warns
about nothing. A path git already tracks is not ignored.

- **Units:** `u1-ignored-output`.
- **Evidence (before):** r1 N8 (2026-10-06): with `.cursor/` ignored, `init` and `sync` exited 0 in silence; a fresh
  clone's `check` reported 25 ledgered files missing and named `sync`, which writes them where git ignores them again;
  with `.claude/` ignored, 59, and `.claude/settings.json` never reached a commit. Only the worktree lane calls
  `check-ignore` (`src/worktree/git.ts:425-458`).
- **Proof:** `test/cli/engine/gitStatus.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/initApply.test.ts`,
  `test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`, `test/docsPages.test.ts`; QA.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-044**):
- GIVEN `.gitignore` line 3 `.cursor/` WHEN `init -y --tools claude,cursor` runs THEN it exits 0, writes the files, and
  warns once naming `.gitignore:3 (.cursor/)`.
- GIVEN that repository's fresh clone WHEN `check` runs THEN it exits 1, `ignored-output` names `.cursor/hooks.json` and
  `.gitignore:3`, and the first next step is the `.gitignore` edit.
- GIVEN a matching path that git tracks THEN the row passes.
- GIVEN no git THEN the row is a warning beginning `skipped —`.

#### REQ-FLOW-045 — Every verb finds the setup from any folder inside the repository (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-repo-root`. `sync`, `check`, `clean`, `config`, `add`, `validate`, `plugin status`,
`learn`, every `handoff` mode and `ledger` use the setup in the nearest folder at or above the working directory that
holds `.stamity/manifest.json`, stopping at the git top-level, and say on stderr when they walked. With no git they use
the working directory. `init` and `plugin setup` refuse when a folder above the working directory, up to the git
top-level, already holds a setup, naming it; `init --force` creates the nested setup anyway. A refusal for want of a
setup names the git top-level as where `init` belongs, never the working directory.

- **Units:** `u1-repo-root`.
- **Evidence (before):** r3 C9.2: from `src/deep`, `learn capture` and `handoff prepare` exited 1 and told the agent to
  run `init`; following that wrote a second setup under `src/deep/`; `handoff list` and `prune` answered zero. Every verb
  reads `ctx.app.runtime.cwd` as the root (`src/cli/commands/learn.ts:444`, `handoff.ts:260`, `sync.ts:141`,
  `check.ts:2090`, …).
- **Proof:** `test/cli/engine/setupRoot.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/repoRoot.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/learn.test.ts`,
  `test/cli/commands/handoff.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/init.test.ts`.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-045**):
- GIVEN an initialised repository WHEN `learn capture` and each `handoff` mode run in `src/deep/` THEN each acts on the
  root's `.stamity/`.
- GIVEN the same WHEN `init -y` runs in `src/deep/` THEN it exits 1 naming the root's manifest and writes nothing.
- GIVEN the same WHEN `sync`, `check`, `config get tools`, `validate`, `clean --dry-run`, `plugin status` and `ledger`
  run there THEN each reports on the root's setup.
- GIVEN a nested git repository THEN the walk stops at its top-level.

#### REQ-FLOW-046 — `sync` keeps its backups out of the working tree, and `clean` keeps them (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, units `u1-import-config-round-trip` and `u1-clean-keeps-state`. Every backup `sync` takes — a
hand-edited generated file it regenerates, a block repair, a forced overwrite, a co-owned document's reclaim — lands
under `.stamity/backups/` at the file's repository-relative path plus `.bak` (or `.bak.<8 hex>`), in a folder that ignores
itself through its own `.gitignore`, so no backup appears in `git status` and the root `.gitignore` gains no line. `sync
--json` names each backup repository-relative in `wrote[].backupPath`. `init` does the same except for an import
`replace`, whose original stays beside the file and is recorded (REQ-FLOW-027). `clean -y` keeps `.stamity/backups/`;
`clean -y --purge` removes it.

- **Units:** `u1-import-config-round-trip`, `u1-clean-keeps-state`.
- **Evidence (before):** r1 C5.e (2026-10-06): after a hand edit, `sync -y` left `.claude/agents/stamity-creator.md.bak`
  and then `.bak.95a5ac91` beside the file, untracked and not ignored; `sync --json` named the backup only inside a
  warning string, as an absolute path (`src/merge/atomicWrite.ts:1471-1478`, `src/merge/safeWrite.ts:1197-1218`).
- **Proof:** `test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts`, `test/merge/bakEscape.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/clean.test.ts`;
  QA.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-046**):
- GIVEN a hand edit to a generated file WHEN `sync -y` runs THEN its backup is
  `.stamity/backups/<path>.bak`, no `*.bak*` exists outside `.stamity/backups/`, and `git status --porcelain` names no
  backup.
- GIVEN `sync --json` THEN `wrote[].backupPath` is that repository-relative path.
- GIVEN a symlinked `.stamity/backups` THEN the overwrite is refused and the file is untouched.
- GIVEN backups present WHEN `clean -y` runs THEN they are byte-identical afterwards; WHEN `clean -y --purge` runs THEN
  they are gone.

### C. `docs/specs/enterprise-upstream-lane.md`

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-020 — The lane closes its own superseded issues (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-lane-issues-freshness`. The lane opens an issue per tag when it cannot push
(`Upstream <tag> needs conflict resolution`, `Upstream <tag> needs a reviewed push`) and identifies its issues by the
body marker `<!-- stamity-upstream-lane: <tag> <kind> -->`. Once the integrated release is the same as or newer than an
open issue's tag — a newer release landed, or the fork is up to date — the workflow closes that issue with a comment
naming the integrated release. It closes only issues that carry the marker, and never one whose tag is newer than the
integrated release. A second run closes nothing more (invariant 6).

- **Units:** `u1-lane-issues-freshness`.
- **Evidence (before):** the lane opens and updates issues and never closes one
  (`.github/workflows/upstream-update.yml:1214-1243`); the marker is written at `:879` and `:1143`, and the lookup reads
  open issues. The enterprise closed four such issues, v1.8.0 to v1.10.0, by hand.
- **Proof:** `test/ci/upstreamWorkflow.test.ts`, executing the workflow's shell against a scripted `gh`; the guide.

Criteria (an `#### Acceptance` subsection under the requirement, as the requirement before it carries, because they run the
workflow's shell against a scripted `gh`):
- GIVEN open lane issues for `v1.8.0`, `v1.9.0` and `v1.11.0` and an integrated release `v1.10.0` WHEN the workflow runs
  THEN the `v1.8.0` and `v1.9.0` issues are closed with a comment naming `v1.10.0`, and the `v1.11.0` issue stays open.
- GIVEN an open issue titled like a lane issue but without the marker THEN it stays open.
- GIVEN the same inputs WHEN the workflow runs a second time THEN no issue is closed, commented on or edited.
- GIVEN a `gh` call that fails while closing THEN the run reports the issue it could not close and exits non-zero.

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-021 — `status --json` reports freshness a heartbeat can read (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-lane-issues-freshness`. `node scripts/upstream.mjs status --json` adds four facts beside
the outcome: `fetchedAt` (when the refs it read were fetched, ISO 8601 UTC), the newest upstream release, the integrated
release, and how many releases the fork is behind. The guide carries a heartbeat recipe that reads them and names the
three signals an enterprise asked for: the lane stalled, a tag moved, a release left unadopted. The unit fixes the other
three field names and the test pins them.

- **Units:** `u1-lane-issues-freshness`.
- **Evidence (before):** `status --json` (`scripts/upstream.mjs:37`, `:83-118`) carries no fetch time and no freshness
  field.
- **Proof:** `test/upstream/lane.test.ts` over the suite's temporary repositories; the guide's recipe pinned in
  `test/docsPages.test.ts`.

Criteria (appended to the fixture list under `## Acceptance criteria`):
- GIVEN the suite's fixture fork at `v1.0.0` and upstream releases up to `v1.3.0` WHEN `status --json` runs online THEN
  the document names `v1.3.0` as the newest release, `v1.0.0` as integrated, a behind count equal to the release tags
  newer than the integrated one (4 there: `v1.1.0`, `v1.1.1`, `v1.2.0`, `v1.3.0`), a `movedTags` list (empty there), and
  a `fetchedAt` within the run's start and end times.
- GIVEN a fork at `v1.3.0` THEN the behind count is 0 and the outcome is `up-to-date`.
- GIVEN `status --json --offline` THEN `fetchedAt` is not the run's own time; it names the last recorded fetch or is
  `null` (the unit states which, and the guide says it).
- GIVEN the guide's heartbeat recipe THEN each command in it is one the lane runs, copied from a run.

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief) — REQ-UPSTREAM-021 — plan-text amendment (ADDED by this file at `:559-581`):**
- After the paragraph's second sentence (`:561-563`, ending "how many releases the fork is behind."), insert: "With `--releases-file <path>` — the upstream's GitHub releases as the guide's
  recipe fetches them — the facts count only published, non-draft releases that carry an asset, dated by
  `published_at`, and `freshness.basis` says so; without it they count tags dated by their `creatordate`
  (`basis: "tags"`), which a failed release run or a tag cut on an old commit misreads."
- Criteria, added: "GIVEN `status --json --releases-file <f>` where one candidate is published with assets, one is a draft
  and one has no asset THEN the behind count is 1, the oldest unintegrated release is dated by its `published_at`, and
  the other two are listed as unpublished." and "GIVEN `freshness.movedTags` THEN each row carries `accepted`."

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-024 — The lane derives the files that carry a fork's identity (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, units `u1-lane-package-files` and `u1-fork-docs-identity`. With the configuration key
`identityFiles` (`{ "suffix": "<id>" }`, the suffix optional), `integrate` derives `package.json`, `package-lock.json`
and the hand-written pages `scripts/identity-files.mjs` lists from the release's copy and the fork's identity instead
of merging them as text, and only where the fork changed nothing else in them:

- `package.json` is the release's copy with each fork-owned key the fork set differently from the merge base taken
  from the fork — the identity keys `scripts/fork-identity.mjs` writes (`name`, `repository`, `homepage`, `bugs`,
  `stamity.publisher`, `private`, `publishConfig`) and every key under `stamity.distribution` — and `version`
  `<X.Y.Z>-<suffix>.<N>`, where `X.Y.Z` is the release's version and N is the fork's N + 1 when its version already
  carries that base and suffix, else 1 (without a suffix, the release's version);
- `package-lock.json` is the release's copy with its root `name` and `version` (top level and `packages[""]`) set to
  the derived pair: a re-lock with no npm and no network;
- a listed page is the release's copy with the fork's identity applied by `fork-identity.mjs`'s four rewrite rules.

The two package files are derived together or not at all: when the fork's `package.json` differs from the merge base
outside the fork-owned keys, its lock differs outside the root identity, or the release's version is a prerelease,
both stay exactly as git left them and the report names the first differences, so a fork that changed its dependencies
keeps today's hand path. A page is derived when the fork's copy equals the base copy or the base copy with the identity
applied. Derived paths are staged, marked `resolvedBy: derivation` in the record and listed in the document's `derived`
rows; a merge whose only conflicts are generated or derived paths finishes without a person. Without `identityFiles`
nothing changes.

Invariant 4 (`:126-129`) gains a sentence: "With `identityFiles`, the files that carry the fork's identity are derived
from the release's copy and that identity, and only when the fork changed nothing else in them, so the fork's side holds
nothing the derived file loses (invariant 5)."

- **Evidence (before):** neither file appears in `scripts/upstream.mjs`, and the guide's `generatedPaths` omits both
  (`docs/enterprise-forks.md:297-303`); measured 2026-10-06, a version bump leaves both `UU` and `fork-identity.mjs`
  refuses mid-merge (`scripts/fork-identity.mjs:189`, `:337`); every release from v1.7.0 to v1.11.0 stopped there in the
  enterprise's fork.
- **Proof:** `test/upstream/identityFiles.test.ts`; `test/upstream/lane.test.ts` over a fixture whose releases bump both
  files and a listed page.

Criteria (appended to the fixture list under `## Acceptance criteria`):
- GIVEN a fork at `v1.0.0` whose `package.json` and lock differ from upstream's only by its identity and `1.0.0-acme.1`,
  with `identityFiles: { "suffix": "acme" }`, WHEN `integrate` runs for `v1.1.0`, which bumps both files, THEN the outcome
  is `integrated`, the merge commit's `package.json` carries the fork's identity, `1.1.0-acme.1` and upstream's
  dependencies, its lock equals upstream's outside the root pair, and the record marks both paths
  `resolvedBy: derivation`.
- GIVEN the same fork without `identityFiles` THEN the outcome is `conflict` naming both paths.
- GIVEN a fork that added a dependency THEN both files hold git's merge result, the outcome is `conflict`, and a message
  names the dependency.
- GIVEN a fork already at `1.1.0-acme.1` WHEN the same base is integrated again THEN the derived version is
  `1.1.0-acme.2`.
- GIVEN a listed page the fork rewrote with its identity, and a release that adds a line naming the canonical package
  beside it, THEN the merged page names the fork's package on both lines and carries no marker.

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-025 — A fork writes and checks its lane configuration from a template upstream ships (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-lane-config`. Upstream ships the recommended configuration as
`scripts/upstream-config.template.json`, outside `.stamity/`, so it arms nothing and the canonical repository stays a
non-fork (the lane's arming rule). `node scripts/fork-identity.mjs <identity arguments> --lane-config` writes
`.stamity/upstream.json` from it: the template's gates, `regenerate`, `generatedPaths` and `watch`, an identity gate
ahead of `check` (`npm ci --ignore-scripts && node scripts/fork-identity.mjs <the same arguments> --lane-config --check`)
and `identityFiles` with the suffix. A rerun on an existing file adds what the template has and the file lacks and
keeps every key and entry of the fork's; `--check` exits 1 naming the file when the identity gate or a template entry is
missing or different. The fork guide's recommended block is that render for the guide's sample identity.

- **Evidence (before):** the block is hand-copied (`docs/enterprise-forks.md:283-307`) with one gate (`:287`); the
  enterprise's older copy lacked `docs/measurements.md`, which joined the list on 2026-09-23 (`3fa99f9c`).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts`; `test/upstream/lane.test.ts` (the template parses); `test/docsPages.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN a fork with no `.stamity/upstream.json` WHEN `--lane-config` runs THEN the file parses with the lane's own
  `parseConfig`, its first gate is the identity gate for those arguments, and its second is `check`.
- GIVEN a file missing a template `generatedPaths` entry THEN `--check` exits 1 naming the file, and a rerun adds the
  entry and keeps the fork's own keys and entries.
- GIVEN the guide THEN its recommended block equals the render for the guide's sample identity.
- GIVEN the canonical repository THEN it carries no `.stamity/upstream.json`, and the workflow's probe ends with its
  notice.

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-026 — `publish` can run on a per-run GitHub App token (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-lane-app-token`. When the repository variable `STAMITY_UPSTREAM_APP_CLIENT_ID` and the
secret `STAMITY_UPSTREAM_APP_KEY` are both set, `publish` mints an installation token at the start of the job with the
GitHub-owned `actions/create-github-app-token`, pinned to a full commit SHA, with `owner` and `repositories` naming this
repository and its permissions narrowed to Contents, Pull requests and Issues write — what its push, pull-request and
issue steps use, never Workflows or Administration. The token is the checkout's push credential and every `gh` step's
`GH_TOKEN`, and is revoked when the job ends. Without the App the job uses `STAMITY_UPSTREAM_TOKEN`, then the per-run
repository token, as before; one App value without the other fails the job before any push, naming both. `probe` and
`prepare` read neither.

- **Evidence (before):** one credential expression (`.github/workflows/upstream-update.yml:446`, `:475`); the App only
  in prose (`:51-52`; this spec `:456-458`).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/upstreamWorkflow.test.ts`.

Criteria (an `#### Acceptance` subsection under the requirement, because they parse the workflow and run its shell):
- GIVEN the workflow THEN the mint step precedes the checkout, is pinned to a 40-hex SHA with its exact version comment,
  sets `owner` to `${{ github.repository_owner }}` and `repositories` to the name the credential step derived from
  `GITHUB_REPOSITORY`, and requests exactly the three write permissions.
- GIVEN the credential step with both App values THEN it answers `app`; with one THEN it exits 1 naming both and printing
  neither value; with only the personal token THEN `token`; with nothing THEN `repository`.
- GIVEN `publish` THEN every step that runs `gh` takes `GH_TOKEN` from the App token, then the personal token, then the
  repository token; no job-level `GH_TOKEN` exists; `prepare` reads no secret and no App variable.

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-027 — A moved release tag stops the lane (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-lane-issues-freshness`. Versions are immutable. A release tag the fork already integrated
— named by an integration record's `releaseCommit`, or by a record's `coveredCommits` for a release its merge covered —
that now names another commit is a moved tag. `status`, `preview` and `integrate` then report the outcome `tag-moved`
(exit 1) and merge nothing, naming each tag with both commits and whether the new one contains the old; `continue`,
`validate` and `abort` are unaffected. A fork accepts a move it reviewed with the configuration key `acceptedTagMoves`
(`{ "<tag>": "<the tag's current commit>" }`); a later move of the same tag stops the lane again. `status --json`'s
`freshness.movedTags` carries every moved tag with `accepted`. The GitHub workflow treats `tag-moved` as it treats
`ancestry-lost`: a red run, no pull request and no issue.

- **Evidence (before):** the tag fetch is forced (`scripts/upstream.mjs:1017`), so a moved tag is followed silently;
  upstream re-pointed `v1.11.0` after its first release run failed (`.stamity/runs/2026-09-30_release-1-11-0/record.md:7`).
- **Proof:** `test/upstream/lane.test.ts`; `test/ci/upstreamWorkflow.test.ts`.

Criteria (appended to the fixture list):
- GIVEN a fork that integrated `v1.1.0` and an upstream that moved `v1.1.0` to another commit WHEN `status` runs THEN the
  outcome is `tag-moved` with exit 1 and a message naming `v1.1.0` and both commits; WHEN `integrate` runs THEN no
  branch, worktree or record is created.
- GIVEN `acceptedTagMoves` naming `v1.1.0` with its current commit THEN `status` reports the release outcome as before and
  the moved row has `accepted: true`; GIVEN it naming another commit THEN `tag-moved`.
- GIVEN a record whose `coveredCommits` names `v1.0.1`, and `v1.0.1` moved, THEN `tag-moved` names `v1.0.1`.
- GIVEN a moved tag the fork never integrated THEN no stop.

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-028 — A fork sets the lane's cadence with a variable, and the docs build stays upstream's (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-lane-issues-freshness`. `on.schedule` takes a static cron, so the hourly cron at minute
17 stays and the probe reads the repository variable `STAMITY_UPSTREAM_SCHEDULE` on scheduled runs: unset, every tick
proceeds; `off`, none does; a comma-separated list of UTC hours from 0 to 23, only the ticks whose slot — the UTC hour of
the run's start less 17 minutes, so a tick that starts up to an hour late keeps its slot — is listed. A skipped tick
ends green with a notice; a dispatch always proceeds; any other value fails the probe naming the variable. The
inherited `docs-site.yml` `build` job runs only in the canonical repository, as its `deploy` job already does.

- **Evidence (before):** the workflow tells forks to edit the cron (`.github/workflows/upstream-update.yml:92`; guide
  `docs/enterprise-forks.md:1179-1180`), and every edit becomes a lane conflict; `docs-site.yml`'s `build` (`:70-77`)
  carries no guard while `deploy` does (`:153-156`).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/upstreamWorkflow.test.ts`; `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`.

Criteria (an `#### Acceptance` subsection):
- GIVEN the cadence step with `STAMITY_UPSTREAM_SCHEDULE=6,18` on a `schedule` event at 06:20 or 07:05 UTC THEN
  `run=true`; at 07:20 THEN `run=false` with a notice; on `workflow_dispatch` at 07:20 THEN `run=true`; with `off` THEN
  `run=false` on every scheduled tick; with `24` or `6;18` THEN exit 1 naming the variable.
- GIVEN `prepare` THEN its condition requires the probe's `run` to be `true`.
- GIVEN `docs-site.yml` THEN `build`'s condition is the canonical-repository guard: true for `zomarit/stamity`, false for
  `acme/stamity-private`.

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-029 — A rulesets-only repository declares its landing policy (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 1, unit `u1-lane-issues-freshness`. Classic branch protection needs Administration: read, which
neither the repository token nor the lane's App token holds, so its read is refused and every lane pull request carries
the not-fully-checked note (`.github/workflows/upstream-update.yml:633-654`). With the repository variable
`STAMITY_LANDING_POLICY` set to `rulesets-only`, an unreadable classic surface is recorded as declared and leaves the
unverified list; the rulesets and repository surfaces are still read and still warn, and a readable classic document
that shows protection overrides the declaration with a warning. Any other non-empty value fails the step naming the
variable.

- **Proof:** `test/ci/upstreamWorkflow.test.ts`.

Criteria (an `#### Acceptance` subsection):
- GIVEN a `gh` stub answering the classic endpoint 403 and merge-permitting rulesets, and `rulesets-only`, THEN
  `checked=true`, `classic=declared` and no note; without the variable THEN the note, as today.
- GIVEN the variable and a classic document with linear history required THEN the linear-history warning and a warning
  that the declaration is contradicted.
- GIVEN `STAMITY_LANDING_POLICY=rulesets` THEN the step exits 1 naming the variable.

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-011 — Landing policy: detected on GitHub, derived everywhere (MODIFIED, appended sentence)

## Units

### u1-npm-dist-tag — a fork release always publishes under an explicit npm dist-tag, and the rehearsal runs the same npm check

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u1-npm-dist-tag |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-031, REQ-PLUGIN-050 |
| `files` | `.github/workflows/fork-release.yml` (header `:4-11`; `probe` outputs `:83-89` and its step `:91-163`; `publish` step "Publish to the registry" `:635-666`; job `dry-run-summary` `:799-847`), `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`, `test/ci/workflow.test.ts` (the case at `:4049-4064` only), `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (header `:5`; identity block `:132-135` and the paragraph after it `:137-142`; "Arm the workflow" `:923-931`; "Tag a release" `:945-983`), `test/docsPages.test.ts` (`REATTESTATION_DATE` `:597`; the identity-block pin `:2284-2286`; one new case), `CHANGELOG.md` (`## [Unreleased]`, `### Fixed`) |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** The publish step runs `npm publish "./$TARBALL" --registry "$REGISTRY"` (`fork-release.yml:666`) with env `NODE_AUTH_TOKEN`, `REGISTRY` (`${{ needs.probe.outputs.registry }}`), `PACKAGE_NAME`, `VERSION` (`:645-649`), after the credential check (`:652-655`) and an `npm view "$PACKAGE_NAME@$VERSION" dist.integrity` idempotence check (`:656-665`). Its npm is Node 24's bundled npm 11 (`Set up Node` `:629-633`, `node-version: '24'`, `registry-url` from the probe). npm 11.21.0 throws "You must specify a tag using --tag when publishing a prerelease version." when the version has a prerelease part and no `--tag` was given on the command line (`lib/commands/publish.js:125-132`: `isDefaultTag = npm.config.isDefault('tag') && !manifest.publishConfig?.tag`); every guide-shaped fork version (`1.11.0-acme.1`) is a prerelease; an explicit `--tag latest` satisfies the check (probe `pub/n11-taglatest.log`, exit 0; without `--tag`, `pub/npm11-notag.log`, exit 1). The rehearsal job `dry-run-summary` (`:803-847`, `permissions: {}`, `timeout-minutes: 5`) runs no npm, so a rehearsal stays green while the real publish fails. The probe step reads `vars.*` through env `ARM`, `REGISTRY`, `BRANCH`, `REPO` (`:93-97`), refuses a bad registry without printing it (`:122-142`) and a bad branch (`:144-156`), then writes `armed=true`, `branch=…`, `registry=…` (`:158-162`); job outputs `armed`, `branch`, `registry` (`:83-89`). The test locks the defect in: `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts:997-1001` expects the calls `[VIEW, "publish ./<tgz> --registry https://npm.pkg.github.com"]` from a stub npm (block `:963-1028`; `FORK_VERSION = "1.10.0-acme.1"` at `:85`). The update notice reads `latest` (`src/cli/notice/updateNotice.ts:270`, `${base}/${encodeURIComponent(packageName)}/latest`). **New, probe.** Env gains `DIST_TAG_INPUT: ${{ vars.STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG }}`, the only read of that variable in the file. After the branch check (`:156`) and before the armed block: `DIST_TAG="${DIST_TAG_INPUT:-}"`, empty → `latest`; accepted only when it matches `^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9._-]*$` and is none of `[vV][0-9]*`, `x`, `X`, `x.*`, `X.*` (shapes semver reads as a version or range; `npm dist-tag add` refuses those, npm 11.21.0 `lib/commands/dist-tag.js:101-102`, but `npm publish --tag` does not check, so the workflow must); otherwise `::error title=Fork release misconfigured::STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG must be an npm dist-tag: a letter, then letters, digits, '.', '_' or '-', and not a version or a range such as v1 or x. Unset it to publish under latest. Its value is not printed.` and exit 1 with no output written. The armed block also writes `dist_tag=$DIST_TAG`; job output `dist_tag: ${{ steps.arm.outputs.dist_tag }}`. The header (`:4-11`) names the fourth, optional variable. **New, publish.** Env gains `DIST_TAG: ${{ needs.probe.outputs.dist_tag }}`; after the credential check, an empty `DIST_TAG` fails: `::error::The probe job named no npm dist-tag, and npm 11 refuses a suffixed version without one. Refusing to publish.` (exit 1, before any npm call); the publish line becomes `npm publish "./$TARBALL" --registry "$REGISTRY" --tag "$DIST_TAG"`; the step comment (`:635-643`) gains one sentence on the tag. **New, gates.** After `Pack tarball`, a step `Rehearse the registry publish` — env `REGISTRY: ${{ needs.probe.outputs.registry }}`, `DIST_TAG: ${{ needs.probe.outputs.dist_tag }}`, `TARBALL: ${{ steps.pack.outputs.tarball }}`; run: `set -euo pipefail`; the empty-`DIST_TAG` refusal; the npm-major check (below 11 → `::error::The rehearsal needs npm 11 or newer, the npm that refuses a prerelease without --tag; this runner has npm <version>.`, exit 1); `echo "npm $(npm --version); dist-tag $DIST_TAG"`; `npm publish "./$TARBALL" --dry-run --registry "$REGISTRY" --tag "$DIST_TAG"` (the behaviour without a credential and with an unreachable packument as described below). Without a credential npm 11 warns "This command requires you to be logged in to … (dry-run)" and still exits 0; it reads the packument and tolerates a failed read (`publish.js:254-280`, `#registryVersions` catches; probe log `npm-cache/_logs/2026-10-01T16_08_25_320Z-debug-0.log:28-37`: GET 404, exit 0); its existing-version check skips prerelease versions (`:265-272`). **dry-run-summary** keeps its one step, `Report what a real run would publish`, with env `DIST_TAG` and the row `\| dist-tag \| \`$DIST_TAG\` \|`; `permissions: {}` and `timeout-minutes: 5` stay; it downloads nothing. **Follows:** `fork-release.yml:86-89` (a value read once in the probe and handed on as an output, so an edit while the run waits cannot split the proved value from the used one) and `:122-142` (a refusal that names the variable and never prints its value). **Test pins that move** (each with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` comment naming this unit): `:232` `expect(pins.length).toBe(7)` stays (the rehearsal adds no action); `:544` the armed outputs gain `dist_tag: "latest"`; `:1000` the publish call gains `--tag latest`; `:204-224` the header phrases gain `STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG`; `test/ci/workflow.test.ts:4049-4064` — `npm publish` now also appears in `fork-release.yml:gates`; the closed list stays `["fork-release.yml:publish","release.yml:publish"]` for the jobs whose `npm publish` lines lack `--dry-run`, and the `gates` line is admitted only because every `npm publish` line in that job carries `--dry-run` and the job holds `contents: read` and reads no `secrets.`. No canonical spelling is added (the census `test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts:215` stays). **Docs** (`docs/enterprise-forks.md`): (a) `:134` becomes `npx -y npm@11 install --package-lock-only --ignore-scripts`, and `:137-142` says why: npm 10 drops the 38 `libc` fields this lockfile records (measured 2026-10-01: npm 10.9.8 left 0 and changed 38 entries; npm 11.21.0 kept all 38 and changed only the root identity); the pin at `test/docsPages.test.ts:2284-2286` (`toContain("npm install --package-lock-only")`, which the npm 11 form no longer contains) becomes `toContain("npx -y npm@11 install --package-lock-only")`; (b) `:923` "Then set the three variables" → "Then set the variables", and after `:930` a row `\| \`STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG\` \| repository variable, optional \| The npm dist-tag a release publishes under. The default is \`latest\`, the tag \`npx\` installs and the CLI's update notice reads, so leave it unset unless you run a separate channel. A value npm would not take as a tag, or one that reads as a version or a range such as \`v1\`, fails the run, which names the variable and never prints the value. \|` (the pin at `test/docsPages.test.ts:2292-2315` then finds every name the workflow reads); (c) "Tag a release" (`:945-953`): the prose says to set the suffixed version, regenerate what carries it, run the gate, commit and tag, and the sh block becomes `npm pkg set version=1.11.0-acme.1`, `npx -y npm@11 install --package-lock-only --ignore-scripts`, `node scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs`, `node scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs`, `npm run build && node dist/cli.js sync`, `npm run check`, `git commit -am "chore(release): v1.11.0-acme.1"`, `git tag v1.11.0-acme.1`, `git push origin HEAD v1.11.0-acme.1`; one sentence after it: the generators and the sync rewrite the files that carry the version (the probe's version-only run failed `test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginManifests.test.ts` and showed a 9-file `check` drift until they ran), and npm 11 refuses a prerelease without a dist-tag, so the workflow always passes one; a new `test/docsPages.test.ts` case pins that block's `npx -y npm@11 install --package-lock-only` and `node dist/cli.js sync`; (d) `:963` "reads the three variables" → "reads the variables"; (e) the `gates` bullet (`:967-973`): it also runs `npm publish --dry-run` with the same Node, npm, registry and dist-tag as `publish`, on every run and before the approval wait, so a rehearsal fails where a real run would; the `dry-run-summary` bullet (`:980-981`) is unchanged. Header `:5` and `REATTESTATION_DATE` per the hand-page rule. **CHANGELOG** `### Fixed`: "**A fork release publishes its suffixed version.** `fork-release.yml` published without `--tag`, and npm 11 refuses a prerelease version without a dist-tag — and every `1.11.0-acme.1`-shaped fork version is one — so the first real fork release failed at the registry while its rehearsal, which never ran npm, stayed green. The publish step now always passes a dist-tag, `latest` unless the new repository variable `STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG` names another, and every run, a rehearsal included, runs `npm publish --dry-run` with the same npm, registry and tag before the approval." |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** the probe step run with `ARM=REPO=<fork>`, `REGISTRY=https://npm.pkg.github.com` and `DIST_TAG_INPUT` unset, **when** it exits, **then** the status is 0 and the outputs are exactly `{armed:"true", branch:"main", registry:"https://npm.pkg.github.com", dist_tag:"latest"}`; with `DIST_TAG_INPUT=acme`, **then** `dist_tag` is `acme`. **Given** `DIST_TAG_INPUT` in {`1.2`, `v1`, `V2.0`, `x`, `X.1`, `-next`, `next tag`, `a/b`, `next!`}, **when** the probe runs armed, **then** it exits 1, prints `::error` naming `STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG`, writes no output and its output does not contain the value. **Given** the workflow source, **then** `vars.STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG` occurs exactly once (the probe's env), and the publish and rehearsal steps' `DIST_TAG` both equal `${{ needs.probe.outputs.dist_tag }}`. **Given** the publish step run with the suite's stub `npm` extended to exit 1 with npm 11's "You must specify a tag using --tag when publishing a prerelease version." for `publish` without `--tag` on a version containing `-`, **when** `NPM_VIEW` is empty, **then** the step exits 0 and the calls are `[VIEW, "publish ./acme-corp-stamity-1.10.0-acme.1.tgz --registry https://npm.pkg.github.com --tag latest"]`; with `DIST_TAG=acme` the last call ends `--tag acme`; with `DIST_TAG=""` the step exits 1 naming the dist-tag and makes no npm call. **Given** `gates`, **then** `Rehearse the registry publish` follows `Pack tarball`, holds the job's only `npm publish` line, and that line carries `--dry-run`, `--registry "$REGISTRY"` and `--tag "$DIST_TAG"`; the job's permissions stay `{ contents: read }` and it reads no `secrets.`; **given** `dry-run-summary`, **then** its steps are exactly `["Report what a real run would publish"]`, its permissions are `{}`, and its report carries the dist-tag row. **Given** the rehearsal step run with the stub npm (answering `--version` with `11.6.2`), `TARBALL`, `REGISTRY`, `DIST_TAG=latest`, **then** it exits 0 and the publish call is `publish ./<tgz> --dry-run --registry https://npm.pkg.github.com --tag latest`; with a stub answering `10.9.8`, **then** it exits 1 naming npm 11; with a stub that exits 1 on `publish`, **then** the step exits 1. **Given** the real npm on PATH reports major 11 or more (the suite's Node 24 legs; skipped below 11 and on Windows, with the reason in the title), **when** a fixture package `1.0.0-acme.1` is packed in a temp dir and the rehearsal step's body runs on it with `REGISTRY=http://127.0.0.1:9`, `npm_config_fetch_retries=0` and a temp npm cache, **then** it exits 0 and prints `with tag latest` and `(dry-run)`; **when** `npm publish <tgz> --dry-run --registry http://127.0.0.1:9` runs without `--tag`, **then** it exits 1 and prints "You must specify a tag using --tag when publishing a prerelease version" (the real-npm pair REQ-PLUGIN-031 asks for, with no external network: the refused local port fails the packument read, which npm tolerates). **Given** the conditions evaluated over `TRIGGER_SHAPES` (`:291-328`), **then** the results are unchanged. **Given** `npx vitest run test/ci/workflow.test.ts`, **then** the `npm publish` placement case passes, and it fails on a parsed copy whose rehearsal line drops `--dry-run` (asserted in the case). **Given** `npx vitest run test/docsPages.test.ts`, **then** it passes with the moved header, the constant and the two moved or new pins. |
| `edgeCases` | A fork whose version carries no suffix (`1.11.0` under its own scope) → `--tag latest` is still passed and npm accepts it; no behaviour change. A rerun of a published release → the `npm view` idempotence check exits 0 before `npm publish`; the dist-tag is not re-applied (moving a tag is `npm dist-tag add`, outside this workflow). A rehearsal of a commit whose non-prerelease version is already published on an anonymously readable registry → npm 11's dry run exits 1 "You cannot publish over the previously published versions" (`publish.js:181-183`), the same bump a real run would need; a suffixed version never meets this (the check skips prereleases). `STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG` set to another channel → `npx <pkg>` and the update notice keep reading `latest` (`updateNotice.ts:270`); the variable's row says so. A fork that sets `publishConfig.tag` by hand → `fork-identity.mjs --registry` writes `publishConfig` as `{ registry }` only (`scripts/fork-identity.mjs:168`), so a fork that ran the identity step carries none; a hand-set one may compete with `--tag` (the probe saw the dry run print `latest` despite it, `pub/n11-pctag.log`). The rehearsal runs in `gates`, beside the tarball it packed, so no download under `permissions: {}` is involved. The registry unreachable during the rehearsal → the packument read fails quietly and the dry run still exits 0; only a tag or a manifest npm refuses turns it red. |
| `depends_on` | none |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts test/ci/workflow.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts && npm run lint && npm run typecheck` |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **files, added:** `.github/workflows/fork-release.yml` (`gates`: a step after `Pack tarball` `:323-335`).
- **interfaces, added:** The npm dry run moves into `gates`, because the brief asks for it "before any tag is pushed" and before the approval (F1): `gates` already runs Node 24 (`:195-198`, the publish job's npm 11, `:629-633`), holds `contents: read` and no secret (`:172-173`), packs the tarball itself, and runs on every armed run — a tag push, a real dispatch and a rehearsal — so a registry refusal stops the run before the `fork-release` environment's approval and before anything irreversible, and no artifact download under `permissions: {}` is involved.
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** `gates`, **when** the conditions are evaluated over `TRIGGER_SHAPES`, **then** `Rehearse the registry publish` runs on every armed shape, tag push included (`test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`).
- **edgeCases, added:** A tag-push run whose version npm refuses → `gates` fails before the approval wait; the tag is already pushed, which is why the guide rehearses before tagging (`u1-fork-release-hardening`).
- **Replaces:** the passage of `interfaces` from "**New, dry-run-summary** (`timeout-minutes: 10` with a one-line reason: the tarball download, the Node setup and npm's packument read with its default retries; `permissions: {}` kept)." through "(5) `Report what a real run would publish` — unchanged, plus env `DIST_TAG` and the row `\| dist-tag \| \`$DIST_TAG\` \|`." (both ends quoted exactly; the sentences between them, steps (1) to (4) with the npm 11 behaviour notes, go with it except as kept below) → "**New, gates.** After `Pack tarball`, a step `Rehearse the registry publish` — env `REGISTRY: ${{ needs.probe.outputs.registry }}`, `DIST_TAG: ${{ needs.probe.outputs.dist_tag }}`, `TARBALL: ${{ steps.pack.outputs.tarball }}`; run: `set -euo pipefail`; the empty-`DIST_TAG` refusal; the npm-major check (below 11 → `::error::The rehearsal needs npm 11 or newer, the npm that refuses a prerelease without --tag; this runner has npm <version>.`, exit 1); `echo "npm $(npm --version); dist-tag $DIST_TAG"`; `npm publish "./$TARBALL" --dry-run --registry "$REGISTRY" --tag "$DIST_TAG"` (the behaviour without a credential and with an unreachable packument as described below). **dry-run-summary** keeps its one step, `Report what a real run would publish`, with env `DIST_TAG` and the row `\| dist-tag \| \`$DIST_TAG\` \|`; `permissions: {}` and `timeout-minutes: 5` stay; it downloads nothing."
- **Replaces:** "`test/ci/workflow.test.ts:4049-4064` — `npm publish` now also appears in `fork-release.yml:dry-run-summary`;" → "`test/ci/workflow.test.ts:4049-4064` — `npm publish` now also appears in `fork-release.yml:gates`;", and "the rehearsal is admitted only because every `npm publish` line in it carries `--dry-run`, its job holds `permissions: {}` and reads no `secrets.`" → "the `gates` line is admitted only because every `npm publish` line in that job carries `--dry-run` and the job holds `contents: read` and reads no `secrets.`".
- **Replaces:** "and the rehearsal runs `npm publish --dry-run` with the same npm and tag." (the CHANGELOG bullet) → "and every run, a rehearsal included, runs `npm publish --dry-run` with the same npm, registry and tag before the approval."

### u1-fork-neutral-tests — the six suites that fail only on a fork derive their expectations or say why they skip

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u1-fork-neutral-tests |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-033, REQ-PLUGIN-022 |
| `files` | `test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts` (`:110-134`), `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts` (`:392-404`), `test/ci/downstreamFixture.ts` (`:91-105`), `test/ci/pluginDownstream.test.ts` (`:719-803` removed), `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts` (`:10`, `:27-32` only), `test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts` (imports `:1-17`, header `:19-44`, comment `:63-68`, census comment `:222-226`, `:309-410` removed), `test/emit/sharedCharterIdentity.test.ts` (`:290`, `:317-322`, one new case), `src/cli/commands/check.ts` (`runDriftGate` `:1106-1107`) |
| `interfaces` | **Witness (red first).** The fork probe of 2026-10-01 (identity `@acme-corp/stamity`, repository `acme-corp/stamity-acme`, `--registry https://npm.pkg.github.com`, version `1.11.0-acme.1`, `stamity.distribution.sources.claude = {kind:"github", repo:"acme-corp/stamity-acme", path:"claude"}`) failed 8 cases in 6 files (`fork-probe/gate-vitest.log:1656-1889`); 7 are this unit's, the 8th (`test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts:284`) is `u1-renovate-suffix`'s. Reproduce in the session scratchpad, never in the checkout: copy `git ls-files -z --cached --others --exclude-standard`, symlink `node_modules`, `git init` and commit everything with `-c user.name=… -c user.email=…@example.invalid -c commit.gpgsign=false`, run `node scripts/fork-identity.mjs --repository https://github.com/acme-corp/stamity-acme --registry https://npm.pkg.github.com`, write that version and source into `package.json`, run `node scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs && node scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs`, then `npx vitest run test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts test/ci/pluginDownstream.test.ts test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts test/emit/sharedCharterIdentity.test.ts` → 7 failed before, 0 after. **1. `distributionIdentity.test.ts:110-134`** asserts `committed.stamity?.distribution` toEqual `{branch:"plugin-dist", tagPattern:"plugins/v<version>"}` (`:113-116`) and then that every client's resolved source is `{kind:"git-subdir", url:`${repository}.git`, path:client}` (`:124-133`); a fork with a documented `sources` entry fails both. Split it: (a) `it.skipIf(!canonical().canonical)(canonicalOnly("carries this repository's committed block, the two release dimensions and nothing else"), …)` keeps `:113-116`; (b) a fork-neutral case "derives every source the committed block does not configure from repository.url": for each of `DISTRIBUTION_CLIENTS`, when `committed.stamity.distribution.sources?.[client]` is absent the resolved source equals the derived default (the `:124-133` expression); when present, the resolution did not throw and the resolved `kind` equals the configured `kind ?? "git-subdir"`. `canonical` and `canonicalOnly` come from `test/support/identity.ts:74,165`. **2. `pluginDistribution.test.ts:392-404`** asserts the BUILT Claude catalog's source is git-subdir, while a fork's configured source is `github`. New: the "by default" claim is made on `renderCatalog("claude", defaultIdentity(), VERSION, FIXED_COMMIT)` (`defaultIdentity` at `:229-233` drops the distribution block), expecting `{source:"git-subdir", url:`${repositoryRoute().url}.git`, path:"claude", ref:`plugins/v${VERSION}`}`; and the built catalog's source equals `renderCatalog("claude", buildCatalogIdentity(basePackage, resolveDistributionIdentity(basePackage)), VERSION, FIXED_COMMIT).plugins[0].source` — the build renders the checkout's own configured source, whatever it is. `owner.name`, `version` and the entry's `version` stay asserted on the built catalog. **3. `downstreamFixture.ts:91-105`**: with `identity: true` the copy keeps the checkout's whole `stamity` object (`...pkg["stamity"]`, `:101`), so a fork's `distribution.sources` naming `acme-corp/stamity-acme` leaks into the `acme/stamity-private` fixture (`pluginDownstream.test.ts:373`, red in the probe). New: drop `distribution` from the copied object (`const { distribution: _dropped, ...rest } = …; pkg["stamity"] = { ...rest, publisher: FORK_PUBLISHER }`), which is what the module's own comment says it sets (`:8-14`). Its two `identity: true` callers keep working: `pluginDownstream.test.ts:160` expects the defaults and `pluginDistribution.test.ts:761-765` writes its own block afterwards. **4. `forkIdentityScript.test.ts:27-32`** sets `OWNER = "Acme-Corp"`, `SCOPE = "acme-corp"`; on a checkout already scoped `acme-corp` the rename moves no `name` and the companion stays `unchanged` (probe: `:172`, `:205` red). New: `OWNER` = `"Globex-Corp"` when the checkout's publisher or the scope of its package name (`canonical().publisher`, the `@<scope>/` of `canonical().name`) is `acme-corp`, compared case-insensitively, else `"Acme-Corp"`; `SCOPE = OWNER.toLowerCase()`; import `canonical` beside `repositoryRoute` (`:10`). **5. `sharedCharterIdentity.test.ts:290,319`** call `runDriftGate(repo.rootDir, GOLDEN_ENGINE_VERSION)`, whose `planSync` call passes no package name, so the plan falls back to the checkout's (`src/cli/commands/sync/engine.ts:473-476`: `packageName: opts.packageName ?? packageName()`), and on a fork every pinned call in the golden fixture (`GOLDEN_PACKAGE_NAME = "@zomarit/stamity"`, `test/emit/goldenFixture.ts:52`) drifts (25 files on four clients in the probe). New signature: `runDriftGate(rootDir: string, engineVersion: string, opts: Parameters<typeof planSync>[2] = {}): Promise<DriftReport>`, passing `opts` to `planSync(rootDir, engineVersion, opts)` (that type is `{ runner?: GitRunner; packageName?: string; npmChannel?: boolean }`, `engine.ts:437-441`). The CLI's own caller (`check.ts:1177`) and every other caller keep two arguments. The two test calls pass `{ runner: goldenGitRunner, packageName: GOLDEN_PACKAGE_NAME, npmChannel: GOLDEN_NPM_CHANNEL }`, the options `plan()` already passes (`:66-72`). A new case proves the option is read on the canonical tree too: after the upgrade, `runDriftGate(…, { runner: goldenGitRunner, packageName: "@fixture-scope/stamity", npmChannel: true })` reports `clean: false`. `check.ts` is outside the coverage floors (`vitest.config.ts:111-197` bind `src/merge/**` and `src/emit/**`). **6. Retire `STAMITY_FORK_SUITE`.** Two opt-in groups read it and no workflow sets it: `forkIdentity.test.ts:309-410` (a renamed copy running the 19 `IDENTITY_SUITES`) and `pluginDownstream.test.ts:719-803` (a renamed copy building its distribution). `u1-fork-ci-job` runs the whole suite — `pluginDistribution.test.ts`'s build of the checkout's own distribution included — in a renamed, suffixed copy on every pull request. Delete both groups and `IDENTITY_SUITES`; rewrite the `forkIdentity.test.ts` header (`:19-44`: two groups, the spelling census and the marketplace source, and one sentence naming `scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs` as the whole-suite witness), the comment at `:63-68` and the census comment at `:222-226` that cite the opt-in group; drop the imports only those groups used (`spawnSync`, `cpSync`, `mkdirSync`, `symlinkSync`, `dirname` in `forkIdentity.test.ts`; lint names any other). The `deliberate` counts (`:169-228`) do not move: the deleted code holds no canonical spelling, and no edit here adds one. **Follows:** `test/support/identity.ts:5-21` (derive the expected value from the checkout, or gate the case on `canonical()` with the reason in its title) and `test/ci/pluginDownstream.test.ts:424-425` (`canonicalOnly`). |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** the canonical tree, **when** `npx vitest run test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts test/ci/pluginDownstream.test.ts test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts test/emit/sharedCharterIdentity.test.ts` runs, **then** it exits 0 and the canonical-only committed-block case runs rather than skips. **Given** the witness copy above, **when** the five fork-relevant files run there, **then** 0 cases fail (7 before) and the committed-block case reports it skipped with "skipped: this checkout is @acme-corp/stamity". **Given** `sharedCharterIdentity.test.ts`, **then** the upgrade case's two drift gates pass the golden options and the new case's foreign name yields `clean: false`. **Given** `git grep -n STAMITY_FORK_SUITE -- test scripts .github`, **then** it prints nothing (the REQ-PLUGIN-022 amended criterion: no case is skipped on an unset `STAMITY_FORK_SUITE`). **Given** `npm run lint && npm run typecheck`, **then** both exit 0 with no unused import. |
| `edgeCases` | A fork that configures a `sources` entry for every client → case (b) asserts only kinds, and the canonical-only case is skipped with its reason. A fork whose publisher is `globex-corp` → `OWNER` stays `Acme-Corp`; only an `acme-corp` checkout switches. A fork with its own `stamity.distribution.branch` or `tagPattern` → the downstream fixture now presents the defaults to `pluginDownstream`, but `pluginDistribution.test.ts:282` still pins the defaults on the checkout's own build (Follow-ups). `runDriftGate` called with `opts.runner` → the stubbed git seam reaches `planSync`, as `plan()` already does; omitted → today's behaviour. The pull request that deletes the two opt-in groups also adds the fork CI job (same plan file), so no merged state lacks the whole-suite witness. |
| `depends_on` | none |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts test/ci/pluginDownstream.test.ts test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts test/emit/sharedCharterIdentity.test.ts test/cli/commands/check.test.ts && npm run lint && npm run typecheck`, then the witness copy run in the scratchpad |

### u1-secret-shaped-fixtures — test fixtures build their example credentials at run time, and a guard keeps literals out of `test/`

| Field | Content |
|---|---|

The guard file holds no literal of any shape. **Follows:** `test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts:31-35,42` ("Every credential-shaped value is assembled at run time from a prefix and a repeated character") and `scripts/leak-gate.mjs:459-469` (an exemption by path, printed on every run). |

### u1-renovate-suffix — `fork-identity.mjs --suffix <id>` makes the Renovate presets follow suffixed versions; the shipped presets stay strict

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u1-renovate-suffix |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-032, REQ-PLUGIN-028, REQ-PLUGIN-053 |
| `files` | `scripts/fork-identity.mjs`, `renovate/companion.json` (`:4-9`, one key added), `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts`, `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts` (the preset half `:228-307`), `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (header `:5`; the flag list `:144-157`; the presets paragraph `:162-165`), `test/docsPages.test.ts` (the flag loop `:2288-2290`; `REATTESTATION_DATE` only per the hand-page rule), `CHANGELOG.md` (`### Added`) |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** `renovate/plugins.json:15` `versioningTemplate` = `regex:^plugins/v(?<major>\d+)\.(?<minor>\d+)\.(?<patch>\d+)$` (JSON-escaped in the file); `:12` `matchStrings` = `"ref":\s*"(?<currentValue>plugins/v[^"]+)"`, which already captures suffixed refs; `:13` `depNameTemplate`. `renovate/companion.json:4-9`: one rule `{description, matchDatasources:["npm"], matchPackageNames:["@zomarit/stamity"], rangeStrategy:"pin"}`. `scripts/fork-identity.mjs`: `parseArgs` (`:65-85`) knows `--repository`, `--scope`, `--registry`, `--check`, `--help`; `main` (`:306-353`) refuses a bad scope (`:318-321`) or registry (`:322`) with `Refusal`, exit 1; exit 2 is reserved for argument errors (`:312-315`); `plan` (`:218-252`) returns three targets, two of them `retargetPreset` calls (`:237-250`) that each rewrite ONE string value in place (`:199-215`: find `JSON.stringify(current)` once, replace it, re-parse and compare with the expected object, refuse otherwise); the report says `updated <file> (<labels>)`, `unchanged <file>` or, under `--check`, `drift <file> (<labels>)` (`:327-348`; `describe` `:255-257`). Renovate regex versioning: `build` is a fourth numeric part compared like `patch`; a `prerelease` group would make a release unstable (modules/versioning/regex, read 2026-10-01). Renovate's `ignoreUnstable` (default true) moves an already-unstable version only within the same `major.minor.patch` (`configuration-options.md` `## ignoreUnstable`), so `1.10.0-acme.2` → `1.11.0-acme.1` is never proposed without `ignoreUnstable: false`; Renovate's `semver` versioning "does not support ranges" (`versioning/semver/readme.md`), so it cannot serve a `rangeStrategy: pin` rule. The two tests that conflict on a fork: `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts:490-509` (the shipped preset must refuse `plugins/v1.9.0-rc.1`) and `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts:279-287` (the preset must accept the tag the checkout renders — `plugins/v1.11.0-acme.1` on a fork; red in the probe). **New, exported** (the module is import-safe, `isMain` at `:355`): `tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix, suffix, required = false }) → string`; `prefix` regex-escaped; `suffix` undefined → `regex:^${prefix}(?<major>\d+)\.(?<minor>\d+)\.(?<patch>\d+)$`; `suffix` `acme` → `regex:^${prefix}(?<major>\d+)\.(?<minor>\d+)\.(?<patch>\d+)(?:-acme\.(?<build>\d+))?$`. `required: true` drops the group's `?`, so the suffix is mandatory — `regex:^${prefix}(?<major>\d+)\.(?<minor>\d+)\.(?<patch>\d+)-acme\.(?<build>\d+)$` — and a `required` call without a `suffix` throws. This unit calls it with `prefix: "plugins/v"`; file 02's `u2-apm-renovate-preset` reuses it with `required: true` for the `plugins/v` refs in `renovate/apm.json` (shared contract). **New CLI.** `--suffix <id>`: the `parseArgs` value table gains `'--suffix': 'suffix'`; `main` refuses a value that does not match `/^[a-z0-9]+$/` with `Refusal("--suffix must be lowercase letters and digits, the <id> in a version such as 1.11.0-<id>.1.")` (exit 1, nothing written, like `--scope`) before `plan`; USAGE (`:49-53`) and the header comment (`:5-16`) gain the flag. **New targets.** `retargetPreset(relPath, edits)` takes a list of `{ locate, key, label, target, spelling? }`, applies them in order to one text and collects the labels of the edits that changed something; `spelling(value)` defaults to `JSON.stringify(value)` and is `(value) => "\"ignoreUnstable\": " + JSON.stringify(value)` for the boolean, so a key-anchored literal is replaced rather than the first `true` in the file; the re-parse comparison and its refusal stay. `renovate/plugins.json` edits: `depNameTemplate` (unchanged), then `versioningTemplate` → `tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix: "plugins/v", suffix })`. `renovate/companion.json` edits: `matchPackageNames[0]` (unchanged), then `ignoreUnstable` → `suffix === undefined`. A missing `ignoreUnstable` key → "renovate/companion.json carries no ignoreUnstable value where the preset keeps it; restore the file from upstream." **Shipped `renovate/companion.json`** gains `"ignoreUnstable": true` and `"respectLatest": true` after `"rangeStrategy": "pin"` (`:8`) — Renovate's default made explicit, so the script flips a value in place; the npm datasource keeps its default `npm` versioning, which orders `-<id>.<n>` by semver precedence and still reads ranges for `pin`. `renovate/plugins.json` stays byte-identical. **Follows:** `scripts/fork-identity.mjs:199-215` (an in-place, verify-after-edit rewrite that keeps the file's hand formatting). **Test pins that move** (`forkIdentityScript.test.ts`, `TEST CHANGE, justified:`): the exact labels at `:171-172` (`updated renovate/plugins.json (depNameTemplate)`, `updated renovate/companion.json (matchPackageNames)`), `:206-207` (`drift …`), `:354-361`, and the line-endings case `:428-444` become fork-neutral: a helper `presetSuffix(root)` reads the copied `renovate/plugins.json` (`/\(\?:-([a-z0-9]+)\\\.\(\?<build>/` on its `versioningTemplate`); the expected labels are `depNameTemplate`, plus `versioningTemplate` when the copied preset carries a suffix the run does not pass, and `matchPackageNames`, plus `ignoreUnstable` when the copied companion's value is not `true`; the line-endings case passes `--suffix <presetSuffix>` when one exists, so only the bytes differ. On the canonical tree every expectation is today's literal. `test/docsPages.test.ts:2288-2290` loops over `["--registry", "--scope"]` → `["--registry", "--scope", "--suffix"]`. **Docs.** `docs/enterprise-forks.md:144` "Two flags change what it writes" → "Three flags", and a bullet: `--suffix <id>` makes the two presets follow suffixed versions such as `1.11.0-acme.1` — `renovate/plugins.json` then accepts `plugins/v<version>-<id>.<n>` tags beside plain ones, and `renovate/companion.json` lets Renovate move a pinned prerelease to the next one (`ignoreUnstable: false`, because npm and Renovate read a suffixed version as a prerelease); the id is lowercase letters and digits; without the flag both presets stay as this repository ships them, matching a prerelease tag and never proposing it; a fork that publishes under a dist-tag other than `latest` adds `"followTag": "<tag>"` to the companion rule. `:162-165`: the presets carry the identity as data — the tag manager's repository and tag shape, the pinned package and whether a prerelease may replace it. Header and `REATTESTATION_DATE` per the hand-page rule. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**`fork-identity.mjs --suffix <id>` for forks that release suffixed versions.** The shipped Renovate presets accept only plain `plugins/v<major>.<minor>.<patch>` tags and never move a pinned prerelease, so a fork releasing `1.11.0-acme.1` had to patch both, and two upstream suites pinned conflicting expectations about them. With `--suffix acme` the tag rule also accepts `plugins/v<version>-acme.<n>` and the companion pin follows the next suffixed release; without the flag both presets stay strict." |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** `tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix: "plugins/v" })`, **then** it equals the committed `renovate/plugins.json` `versioningTemplate`, read from the repository root, byte for byte. **Given** `tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix: "plugins/v", suffix: "acme", required: true })`, **then** it matches `plugins/v1.11.0-acme.1` and not `plugins/v1.11.0`, and `required: true` without a suffix throws. **Given** `T = tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix: "plugins/v", suffix: "acme" })` without its `regex:` prefix, **then** it matches `plugins/v1.11.0`, `plugins/v1.11.0-acme.1` and `plugins/v1.11.0-acme.12` (whose `build` group is `12`), and does not match `plugins/v1.11.0-rc.1`, `plugins/v1.11.0-other.1`, `plugins/v1.11.0-acme`, `plugins/v1.11.0-acme.1.2`, `plugins/v1.11` or `v1.11.0-acme.1`. **Given** a `fork()` checkout, **when** `fork-identity.mjs --repository <FORK_URL> --suffix acme` runs, **then** it exits 0, `plugins.json`'s `versioningTemplate` equals `T`'s template, `companion.json`'s rule has `ignoreUnstable: false`, and stdout contains `updated renovate/plugins.json (depNameTemplate, versioningTemplate)` and `updated renovate/companion.json (matchPackageNames, ignoreUnstable, respectLatest)`; **when** it reruns with the same arguments, **then** it prints `unchanged` for the three targets and their sha256 stay equal; **when** `--check` runs with the same arguments, **then** it exits 0. **Given** that checkout, **when** the script reruns without `--suffix`, **then** `versioningTemplate` equals the committed value, `ignoreUnstable` is `true`, and stdout names `versioningTemplate` and `ignoreUnstable` among the changed keys. **Given** `--suffix` in {`Acme`, `acme.1`, `ac-me`, `acme_1`, `""`}, **then** the script exits 1, stderr contains `--suffix`, and the three targets' sha256 are unchanged. **Given** `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts`, a new case reads both presets and `package.json`: **then** `versioningTemplate` equals `tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix: "plugins/v", suffix })` for the suffix it carries (or none), the companion's `ignoreUnstable` and `respectLatest` are both `suffix === undefined`, and when the version's prerelease has the form `<x>.<n>`, `x` equals that suffix — so a fork with a suffixed version and strict presets fails with "run node scripts/fork-identity.mjs --suffix <x>"; on the canonical tree (`1.11.0`, strict presets) it passes. **Given** `npx vitest run test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`, **then** `:490-509` passes with no edit. **Given** `npx vitest run test/docsPages.test.ts`, **then** the identity section names `--suffix`. |
| `edgeCases` | A fork that adopts a suffix later → its current pin `plugins/v1.10.0` is still a valid value under the suffix-aware rule (the group is optional), so Renovate keeps updating it. A fork repository that still holds upstream `plugins/v*` tags from an old `--mirror` import → they match the optional group and Renovate may propose them (Risks). A custom `stamity.distribution.tagPattern` → the presets keep `plugins/v`, as today (Follow-ups). `--suffix` with `--check` on a strict tree → `drift` for both presets, nothing written, exit 1. A `companion.json` whose rule lost `ignoreUnstable` in an upstream merge → the refusal names the file and "restore the file from upstream"; nothing is written. The canonical version itself becoming a prerelease (a release candidate) → the new `releaseManifest` case would ask for a suffix; the canonical release never cuts one today (`release.yml` publishes `latest`). |
| `depends_on` | u1-fork-neutral-tests (`test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts`), u1-secret-shaped-fixtures (`test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts`), u1-npm-dist-tag (`docs/enterprise-forks.md`, `test/docsPages.test.ts`, `CHANGELOG.md`) |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts && npm run lint && npm run typecheck` |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **files, added:** `docs/plugins.md` (`:551-558` only), `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (a new `### Version a fork's releases` after the flag list `:144-160`).
- **interfaces, added:** **`respectLatest`.** Renovate's configuration options (read 2026-10-06 by r2, https://docs.renovatebot.com/configuration-options/): `ignoreUnstable: false` "usually" needs `respectLatest: false` "so that Renovate considers versions ahead of `latest`". The shipped `renovate/companion.json` gains `"respectLatest": true` after `"ignoreUnstable": true` (Renovate's default made explicit, so the script flips a value in place); the companion's edits become `matchPackageNames[0]`, then `ignoreUnstable` → `suffix === undefined`, then `respectLatest` → `suffix === undefined`, with the same key-anchored spelling; a missing `respectLatest` key → "renovate/companion.json carries no respectLatest value where the preset keeps it; restore the file from upstream." It matters once a fork publishes under a `STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG` other than `latest`, when `latest` lags the fork's newest release. **Fold of inbox ``"the Renovate presets passage does not say that a fork releasing suffixed versions runs `fork-identity.mjs --suffix`" at 077e8a78``:** `docs/plugins.md:551-558` says the companion pins this repository's own package and the tag rule watches this repository; that a fork releasing suffixed versions runs `fork-identity.mjs --suffix <id>`, after which both presets follow `-<id>.<n>` and the companion moves a pinned prerelease past `latest`; and that a consumer extends the repository it installs from, the canonical example written `github>zomarit/stamity//renovate/plugins.json` and `github>zomarit/stamity//renovate/companion.json` (the extends spelling `u1-fork-docs-identity` rewrites). **Version scheme (F12):** `### Version a fork's releases` states the scheme `<X.Y.Z>-<suffix>.<N>` — N starts at 1 for each new upstream base and counts up for a re-release of the same base, which the guide never said (it shows `1.11.0-acme.1` only, `docs/enterprise-forks.md:953`; the brief's `-<suffix>.1` burned and re-released as `.2` agrees) — and its four consequences, each with its remedy: npm refuses the version without a dist-tag (the workflow always passes one, `STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG`); Renovate needs the suffix-aware presets (`--suffix`); an imported upstream `vX.Y.Z` tag sorts above every `vX.Y.Z-<suffix>.N` (consumer rules name the suffix); the plugin locator accepts a companion at exactly the root's own prerelease. It says the fork probe (`u1-fork-ci-job`) proves the first two on every pull request.
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** `fork-identity.mjs --suffix acme`, **then** the companion carries `respectLatest: false` and stdout names `respectLatest`; **given** a run without `--suffix`, **then** `true`; **given** a companion without `respectLatest`, **then** exit 1 naming it (`test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts`). **Given** `npx vitest run test/docsPages.test.ts`, **then** a new case finds in `### Version a fork's releases` the scheme `<X.Y.Z>-<suffix>.<N>`, `STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG`, `--suffix`, `respectLatest` and the locator sentence, and `docs/plugins.md`'s presets passage names `--suffix`.
- **edgeCases, added:** A consumer that sets its own `followTag` → `respectLatest` no longer decides; the bullet on `followTag` stays.
- Inbox fold (2026-10-08, run 2026-10-08_inbox-pass): ``"the Renovate presets assume the default `plugins/v<version>` tag pattern" at 077e8a78`` — retargets `matchStrings` and the versioning prefix from the resolved pattern.
