**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **files, added:** `scripts/upstream.mjs` (`OUTCOMES` `:186-201`; `CONFIG_KEYS` and `parseConfig` `:244-355`; `buildRecord` `:819-847`; `deriveState` `:1250-1340`; `runStatus` `:1940-1950`; `runPreview` `:1958-1969`; `runIntegrate` `:2155-2168`; `finishMerge`'s record `:2087-2100`; `FLAGS_WITH_VALUE` `:2481`, `parseArguments` `:2484-2510` and `USAGE`), `.github/workflows/upstream-update.yml` (the schedule comment `:91-95`; `probe` outputs `:125-127` and a new step after `:146-186`; `prepare` `if` `:199`; the outcome list `:509-516`; `Check the target branch's landing policy` `:581-690`; the provenance table `:713-727`; the verdict arm `:1235`), `.github/workflows/docs-site.yml` (`build` `:70-77`), `test/ci/workflow.test.ts` (the docs-site describe `:3795`), `test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts` (`:209` only), `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (the outcome table `:391-404`; the key table `:266-279`; `## Land the update branch` `:491-501`; `## Turn on the GitHub workflow` `:1177-1184`).
- **interfaces, added:** **Principle 3 — a moved tag stops the lane.** The fetch is forced (`+refs/tags/*`, `scripts/upstream.mjs:1017`; Package 18's `releaseTagRefspec` keeps the `+`), so today a tag upstream re-points is followed silently (upstream re-pointed `v1.11.0` after its first release run failed, `.stamity/runs/2026-09-30_release-1-11-0/record.md:7`). `deriveState` computes `state.movedTags`: for every record at the target head (`readRecords`, `:1073-1105`) whose `releaseCommit` is an ancestor of the target (not a lost record), and for every entry of its `coveredCommits`, a `{ tag, recordedCommit, currentCommit, accepted }` row when the tag was fetched (`listUpstreamTags` `:1036-1062`, peeled) and names another commit; `accepted` is `config.acceptedTagMoves[tag] === currentCommit`; rows sorted by `compareReleases`. `state.outcome` is `tag-moved` when any row is unaccepted, ahead of the `ancestry-lost` rule (`:1339`). `runStatus` adds one message per unaccepted row: `upstream moved <tag> from <old 7> to <new 7> (<the new commit contains the old one | it does not contain the old one>) after this branch integrated it; versions are immutable, so the lane stops. Confirm the move with upstream, then accept it in .stamity/upstream.json: "acceptedTagMoves": { "<tag>": "<new full sha>" }`. `runPreview` and `runIntegrate` stop right after `fillStateInto` with the same outcome and messages, creating no branch, worktree or record; `continue`, `validate` and `abort` are unaffected. `OUTCOMES` gains `"tag-moved": 1`. `buildRecord` gains `coveredCommits` — `{ <tag>: <commit> }` for each release the merge covers, the target included — so a covered tag that moves later is seen (inbox ``"an integration record keeps a single `releaseCommit`" at 077e8a78``); a record without it covers no tag but its own. New config key `acceptedTagMoves`: absent, or an object mapping a tag `checkTagShape` (`:431`) accepts to a 40- or 64-hex object id, else exit 2 `"acceptedTagMoves" must map release tags to full commit ids`. The workflow: `tag-moved` joins the closed outcome list (`upstream-update.yml:509-516`) and the verdict's `ancestry-lost` arm (`:1235`): a red run, no pull request, no issue. **F9 — published releases.** New option `--releases-file <path>` for `status`, `preview` and `integrate`: a file of the upstream's GitHub releases, JSON Lines or one JSON array of objects with `tag_name` (string), `draft` (boolean), `published_at` (string or null) and `assets` (a count, or the REST API's asset array), read once relative to the working directory; another shape exits 2 naming the file and the entry. With it, `freshness.basis` is `"published-releases"`: a release counts only when it is not a draft, has at least one asset and a `published_at`; `latestRelease` is the newest admitted tag that counts, dated by `published_at`; `integratedRelease.date` is its `published_at` or null; `releasesBehind` counts the candidates that count; `oldestUnintegratedRelease` is the oldest of them, dated by `published_at`; `unpublishedTags` lists the candidate tags that do not count (a failed run's tag, a draft). Without it, `basis` is `"tags"` and every date is the tag's `creatordate` (`:1039`), which a lightweight tag cut today on an old commit misdates (r4 measured `2026-09-02T10:00:00Z` for such a tag). The lane stays git-only (invariant 7): the caller fetches the releases. `LaneDocument.freshness` (`test/upstream/fixtures.ts:567-616`) gains `basis` and `unpublishedTags`, and each `movedTags` row gains `accepted`. **F15 — the cadence.** `on.schedule` takes a static cron (GitHub's "Events that trigger workflows", read 2026-10-06 by r4), so the hourly cron at minute 17 stays and the probe gains `Apply the schedule` (`id: cadence`) after `Read the lane configuration`: env `SCHEDULE: ${{ vars.STAMITY_UPSTREAM_SCHEDULE }}`, `CRON_MINUTE: '17'`; a value other than empty, `off` or `^(?:[01]?[0-9]|2[0-3])(?:,(?:[01]?[0-9]|2[0-3]))*$` → `::error title=Upstream schedule misconfigured::STAMITY_UPSTREAM_SCHEDULE must be off, or a comma-separated list of UTC hours from 0 to 23 such as 6 or 0,12. Nothing ran.`, exit 1; on a `schedule` event with a value, the slot is the UTC hour of the run's start less `CRON_MINUTE` minutes (shell arithmetic over `date -u +%H` and `+%M`), so a tick GitHub starts up to an hour late keeps its slot; `off`, or a slot not listed, writes `run=false` and `::notice title=Scheduled run skipped::…`; else `run=true` (a dispatch always `true`). Probe output `run`; `prepare`'s `if` (`:199`) becomes `needs.probe.outputs.enabled == 'true' && needs.probe.outputs.run == 'true'` (`publish` follows `prepare`'s result). The cron comment (`:92-94`) says the cadence is the variable's. **F15 — the landing declaration.** `Check the target branch's landing policy` gains env `LANDING_POLICY: ${{ vars.STAMITY_LANDING_POLICY }}`: a value other than empty or `rulesets-only` → `::error title=Landing policy misdeclared::STAMITY_LANDING_POLICY must be rulesets-only or unset.`, exit 1 before any API call; with `rulesets-only`, a classic read that leaves `CLASSIC='unchecked'` (`:637-644`) records `CLASSIC='declared'` and adds nothing to `UNCHECKED`; a classic read that returns a document with protection keeps the read result and adds `::warning` that the declaration is contradicted; the rulesets and repository surfaces are read and warn as before; the provenance table gains `| landing policy | rulesets only, declared by STAMITY_LANDING_POLICY |` when declared. **F15 — the docs build.** `docs-site.yml`'s `build` job (`:70-77`) gains `if: github.repository == 'zomarit/stamity'`; it ran on every lane pull request touching `docs/**`, `website/**` or `README.md` in a fork (`:31-37`), while `deploy` was already guarded (`:153-156`). The new `test/ci/workflow.test.ts` case reuses the module constant `CANONICAL_REPOSITORY` (Package 18's, else added here once and reused by `u1-fork-ci-job`); the census at `test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts:209` moves by exactly the literals added, with the reason. **Docs pin.** A new `test/docsPages.test.ts` case for `## Turn on the GitHub workflow`, shaped like the release-section case (`:2293-2315`): every `vars.` or `secrets.` name `upstream-update.yml` reads is named in that section, and every `STAMITY_` name the section prints is read by the workflow.
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** a fork that integrated `v1.1.0` and an upstream fixture that moves `v1.1.0` to a new commit, **when** `status` runs online, **then** the outcome is `tag-moved`, exit 1, and a message names `v1.1.0` with both commits; **when** `integrate` runs, **then** no branch, worktree or record is created; **given** `acceptedTagMoves` naming `v1.1.0` with the new commit, **then** `status` reports the release outcome it reported before the move and `freshness.movedTags[0].accepted` is true; **given** it naming another commit, **then** `tag-moved`; **given** a record whose `coveredCommits` names `v1.0.1` and `v1.0.1` moved, **then** `tag-moved` names `v1.0.1`; **given** a moved tag the fork never integrated, **then** no stop (`test/upstream/lane.test.ts`). **Given** `status --json --releases-file <f>` with `v1.1.0` published with two assets, `v1.2.0` a draft and `v1.3.0` published with none, **then** `basis` is `published-releases`, `releasesBehind` is 1, `oldestUnintegratedRelease` is `v1.1.0` dated by its `published_at`, and `unpublishedTags` is `["v1.2.0", "v1.3.0"]`; **given** a file holding `{`, **then** exit 2 naming the file; **given** no file, **then** `basis` is `tags`. **Given** `acceptedTagMoves` `{ "v1.1.0": "abc" }`, **then** exit 2 naming `acceptedTagMoves`. **Given** the cadence step run with a stub `date` and `GITHUB_EVENT_NAME=schedule`, **then** `SCHEDULE=6,18` at 06:20 and at 07:05 writes `run=true`, at 07:20 `run=false` with the notice; `off` writes `run=false`; on `workflow_dispatch` at 07:20 `run=true`; `24` and `6;18` exit 1 naming the variable; the step's `CRON_MINUTE` equals the cron's minute field (`test/ci/upstreamWorkflow.test.ts`). **Given** the landing step with a stub `gh` answering the classic endpoint 403 and merge-permitting rulesets, **then** with `rulesets-only` it writes `checked=true` and `classic=declared` and no note, and without the variable the note as today; **given** the variable and a classic document with `required_linear_history.enabled: true`, **then** the linear-history warning and the contradiction warning; **given** `rulesets`, **then** exit 1 naming the variable. **Given** the verdict step with `OUTCOME=tag-moved`, **then** exit 1 under the `ancestry-lost` arm, and the validation step accepts the word. **Given** `docs-site.yml`, **then** `build`'s `if` is the guard, true for `zomarit/stamity` and false for `acme/stamity-private` (`test/ci/workflow.test.ts`). **Given** `npx vitest run test/docsPages.test.ts`, **then** the lane-section case passes, the outcome table names `tag-moved`, and the key table names `acceptedTagMoves`.
- **edgeCases, added:** A fork mid-update when its target tag moves → `continue` finishes the merge it holds and records that commit (`:2286-2289`); the next `status` reports `tag-moved`. A tag deleted upstream → not a move; nothing stops. A scheduled tick GitHub starts more than an hour late → its slot is missed; the next listed hour runs (the heartbeat threshold should allow one missed slot). `STAMITY_UPSTREAM_SCHEDULE` on a dispatch → ignored. A repository declaring `rulesets-only` whose classic protection is readable and empty (404 "Branch not protected") → `classic=none` as today; the declaration changes nothing there.
- Inbox fold (2026-10-08, run 2026-10-08_inbox-pass): ``"the close step closes lane issues by semver order" at 077e8a78`` — an ancestry check before a "carried" close.

### u1-fork-ci-job — every pull request runs this tree as a renamed, suffixed fork, merge-blocking in the canonical repository

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u1-fork-ci-job |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-033, REQ-UPSTREAM-017, REQ-PLUGIN-053 |
| `files` | `scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs` (new), `test/ci/forkProbe.test.ts` (new), `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (lane map `:18-75`; a new job between `plugin-route` `:488-663` and the `records` comment `:664`; the aggregator comment `:800-825` and job `:826-848`), `test/ci/workflow.test.ts` (lanes `:246-267`; aggregator pins `:492-537`; the records-only describe `:729-813`; lane map `:838-889`; aggregator executed `:893-988`; page pin `:1737-1752`; one new describe), `test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts` (`:209` only), `CONTRIBUTING.md` (header `:1`; "What CI proves" `:76-111`), `GOVERNANCE.md` (header `:1`; "What `all-ci-checks` covers" `:50-95`), `CHANGELOG.md` (`### Changed`) |
| `interfaces` | **Decision: copy, not worktree.** The probe copies `git ls-files -z --cached --others --exclude-standard` (1,696 files, 34.8 MB at `88fcfd32`, no tracked symlink) into `mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "stamity-fork-probe-"))`, symlinks `node_modules` to the source checkout's (the house pattern for linked worktrees; `.gitignore:1-3` ignores both the directory and the symlink form), and makes it a fresh repository (`git init -q -b main`, `git add -A`, `git -c user.name=fork-probe -c user.email=fork-probe@example.invalid -c commit.gpgsign=false commit -q -m "fork-probe: the imported tree"`). Not `git worktree add`: a worktree carries `HEAD` only, so a local run would silently ignore uncommitted edits (the two retired opt-in groups copied `--cached --others` for exactly that reason, `forkIdentity.test.ts:351-353`); its commits and its registration land in the developer's repository, and a crash leaves a stale `.git/worktrees` entry; and history buys nothing, because the suites that read the checkout's history already skip without it — CI's shallow legs need that (`test/ci/packSigningRehearsal.test.ts:139-146`, `test/evals/manualRunner.test.ts:1436`; after Package 18 the records suite leaves `npm test`). The source checkout is only read: its `git status --porcelain` is the same before and after. **`scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs`** (import-safe ESM, Node built-ins and `git` only; header in the style of `scripts/ci/records-only.mjs:1-15`; entry guard `process.argv[1] && pathToFileURL(resolve(process.argv[1])).href === import.meta.url`, as `:145` there). Usage `node scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs [--keep]`. Exit 0 every step passed; 1 a fork step or a gate step failed, each failure named with its exit code and its last 40 output lines; 2 the probe could not run (an unknown argument, not run from a checkout root, no `node_modules`, the copy or `git init` failed). Exports: `FORK_PROBE_IDENTITY = { repository: "https://github.com/fork-probe/stamity-fork", registry: "https://npm.pkg.github.com", suffix: "fork", scope: "fork-probe" }`; `FORK_PROBE_SOURCES = { claude: { kind: "github", repo: "fork-probe/stamity-fork", path: "claude" } }` (a documented `stamity.distribution.sources` value, `docs/enterprise-forks.md:866-874`, in the shape the enterprise used); `forkVersion(version, suffix) → "<major>.<minor>.<patch>-<suffix>.1"` (prerelease and build metadata dropped; throws on a non-semver input); `REGENERATE_COMMANDS` = the guide's `regenerate` list (`docs/enterprise-forks.md:289-296`) without its first entry, `npm ci --ignore-scripts`, which the borrowed `node_modules` replaces; `npmCommandFor(npmVersion) → ["npm"]` when the major is 11 or more, else `["npx", "-y", "npm@11"]` (npm 11 is the behaviour under test; CI's Node 24 bundles npm 11, the maintainer's Node 22.22.3 has npm 10.9.8); `lockChangesBeyondRoot(before, after)` imported from `scripts/identity-files.mjs` (`u1-lane-package-files`): every `packages` key whose entry differs, the root identity ignored; `parseArgs(argv)`; `runSteps(steps, exec) → { exitCode, results }` over an injected `exec(command, args, cwd, shell) → { status, output, durationMs }`, which runs every gate step even after one failed and stops after a failed fork step. **Fork steps**, in the copy: (1) `node scripts/fork-identity.mjs --repository <repository> --registry <registry> --suffix fork`; (2) commit; (3) write `version = forkVersion(<source version>, "fork")` and `stamity.distribution.sources = FORK_PROBE_SOURCES` into `package.json` with `JSON.stringify(pkg, null, 2) + "\n"` (the file's own serialisation, `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts:136-139`); (4) each of `REGENERATE_COMMANDS` through the platform shell, as the lane runs `regenerate` (`scripts/upstream.mjs:1490-1504`): `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`, `node scripts/generate-docs.mjs`, `node scripts/generate-pack-manifests.mjs`, `node scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs`, `node scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs`, `npm run build && node dist/cli.js sync`; (5) `<npm11> install --package-lock-only --ignore-scripts --no-audit --no-fund`, after which `lockChangesBeyondRoot` must be `[]` (npm 10 would change 38 entries, npm 11 changes none, measured 2026-10-01); (6) commit. **Gate steps**, each its own child without a shell: `npm run gate`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `node scripts/ci/test-run.mjs` (vitest with CI's one re-run of a timeout-only failure, `scripts/ci/test-run.mjs:1-24`), `npm run build`, `npm run knip` — the six steps of `npm run check` (`package.json` `scripts.check`) — then `node dist/cli.js check`, `node scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs --check`, `node scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs --check`, `node scripts/fork-identity.mjs --repository <repository> --registry <registry> --suffix fork --check`, `node scripts/tarball-smoke.mjs` (the fork release's own ladder, `fork-release.yml:309-321`), `npm pack --json --pack-destination <tmp>` then `<npm11> publish <tgz> --dry-run --tag latest --registry <registry>` (must exit 0), then the same command without `--tag` (must exit non-zero and print "You must specify a tag using --tag when publishing a prerelease version" — the real-npm witness that the rule this file guards still holds), and last `git status --porcelain --untracked-files=no` empty (no gate rewrote a tracked file). Output: one `PASS\|FAIL <step> (<seconds>s)` line per step; a markdown table appended to `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` when set; the copy removed unless `--keep`, which prints its path. **`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, at `88fcfd32`.** A job `fork-probe` after `plugin-route`: `name: fork probe (this tree as a renamed, suffixed fork)`, `needs: changes`, `if: github.repository == 'zomarit/stamity' && needs.changes.outputs.records_only != 'true'`, `runs-on: ubuntu-latest`, `timeout-minutes: 20`, `permissions: { contents: read }`; steps `Checkout` (this file's pin, `persist-credentials: false`, depth 1), `Set up Node` (`node-version: '24'`, `cache: npm`), `Install` (`npm ci`), `Fork probe` (`node scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs`). The timeout is derived in a comment: 22 check legs of 11 green `main` runs (2026-09-30 to 10-01, among them 36839397885 and 36837025982) took 234-386 s per job, their coverage test step 206-343 s; this job runs the suite without coverage plus about 2 minutes of identity, regeneration, the dogfood check, the tarball smoke (8 s on the floor leg) and a pack, so about 8.5 minutes at the slowest observed; 20 leaves 2.3x for a loaded runner and the one re-run; re-derive it from the first ten runs, as `plugin-route`'s comment says of itself (`:494-501`). The aggregator: `needs: [changes, check, records, supply-chain, apm-install, plugin-route, fork-probe]`; its step gains `env: REPO: ${{ github.repository }}` (data, never interpolated into the script); the echo line gains `fork-probe=…`; the records-only branch asserts `test "${{ needs.fork-probe.result }}" = "skipped"`; the full branch asserts `success` when `$(printf '%s' "$REPO" \| tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')` is `zomarit/stamity` and `skipped` otherwise. The lane map's MERGE-BLOCKING list gains a `fork-probe` bullet (one leg, canonical only; what the script does; a fork's own CI skips it because a renamed fork is the thing it imitates; names `scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs`), and the aggregator comment one sentence. **After Package 18** (`docs/plans/014-lean-repository-02.md`, unit `r5-ci-records-job`, expected at intake): `changes` and the records-only split are gone and `records` is canonical-only too; `fork-probe` then carries `if: github.repository == 'zomarit/stamity'` and no `needs`, and joins r5's `REPO`-keyed assertion beside `records`: `success` in `zomarit/stamity`, `skipped` elsewhere. **`test/ci/workflow.test.ts`** (`TEST CHANGE, justified:` on each moved pin): the lane list (`:254-263`) gains `"fork-probe"` after `"plugin-route"` and the title says nine lanes; the aggregator `needs` (`:503-510`) gains it, and the report must contain `needs.fork-probe.result` asserted both ways (`:514-536`); `fork-probe` does NOT join `HEAVY` in the records-only describe (`:730`), whose evaluation context carries no repository: a separate case evaluates its condition over the same events and answers with `github.repository` = `zomarit/stamity` (runs exactly when the answer is not `true`) and = `acme/stamity-private` (never runs); the lane map's running lanes (`:847-869`) gain "`fork-probe`" and "scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs"; the executed aggregator (`:902-988`): `Results` gains `"fork-probe"`, `FULL` `"success"`, `SHORT` `"skipped"`, `execute(results, recordsOnly, repo = CANONICAL_REPOSITORY)` spawns with `env: { ...process.env, REPO: repo }`, and new cases — a full run in the canonical repository fails on `fork-probe` failure, cancelled or skipped; a full run in `acme/stamity-private` passes with `skipped` and fails with `success`; records-only fails unless `skipped`; a new describe pins the job (runs-on, timeout 20, permissions, no secret, step names, the `run`, setup-node 24); the page pin (`:1737-1752`) also wants `scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs` in `CONTRIBUTING.md` and `GOVERNANCE.md`. One module constant `CANONICAL_REPOSITORY = "zomarit/stamity"` serves every new case and the job-guard string (reuse r5's if it added one), so the census at `test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts:209` moves by exactly the literals added (one, or none), with the reason. **`test/ci/forkProbe.test.ts`**, unit cases over the exports (no copy, no nested vitest): `REGENERATE_COMMANDS` equals the guide's list, parsed from the json block under "### Start from these values for a fork of this repository", minus its first entry; the gate steps hold each `npm run <x>` of `package.json`'s `scripts.check`, split on `&&`, in order, as separate steps; `forkVersion("1.11.0","fork")` is `"1.11.0-fork.1"`, `forkVersion("1.12.0-rc.1","fork")` is `"1.12.0-fork.1"`, `forkVersion("1.11.0+b.5","fork")` is `"1.11.0-fork.1"`, and `forkVersion("next","fork")` throws; `npmCommandFor("11.6.2")` is `["npm"]` and `npmCommandFor("10.9.8")` is `["npx","-y","npm@11"]`; `lockChangesBeyondRoot` returns `[]` for a root-only change and names the package for a dropped `libc` field; `runSteps` with a fake exec — a failing gate step gives exit 1 and every later gate step still ran; a failing fork step gives exit 1 and no gate step ran; `parseArgs(["--bogus"])` gives exit 2; the probe's owner differs from `FORK_PUBLISHER` (`acme`, `test/ci/downstreamFixture.ts:15`) and from the script test's owners. **Docs.** `CONTRIBUTING.md:80-81` "A full run passes when three lanes pass" → four lanes, the fourth "in this repository only, the fork probe"; a bullet after the plugin route gate describing the copy, the identity, the regeneration, the steps and the dry-run publish, and "Run it locally after `npm ci` with `node scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs`; it takes about as long as the suite." `GOVERNANCE.md:53-54` the same count change, and one paragraph after `:86`. Both headers move to the commit form. **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**Every pull request now proves this repository as a fork.** A merge-blocking CI job copies the tree, renames it with `fork-identity.mjs --suffix fork`, gives it a `<version>-fork.1` version and a documented `stamity.distribution.sources` entry, regenerates it the way the fork guide does, and runs every gate, the dogfood check, the tarball smoke and npm 11's own `publish --dry-run`. The suite under it no longer reads the operator's git configuration, gives its git-heavy cases headroom, and holds no real-format credential in a fixture, so the gate a fork inherits passes as a fork." **Follows:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml:488-502` (`plugin-route`: a one-leg merge-blocking lane with no credential and a derived timeout) and `docs/plans/014-lean-repository-02.md` `r5-ci-records-job` (a canonical-only merge-blocking job asserted through `REPO`). |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** `npx vitest run test/ci/forkProbe.test.ts test/ci/workflow.test.ts test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts`, **then** it exits 0. **Given** a local `npm ci && node scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs` on the tree with this file's other units landed, **then** it exits 0, prints a PASS line for every fork step, gate step and generator check, the copy's `package.json` carries `@fork-probe/stamity`, a version ending `-fork.1` and `stamity.distribution.sources.claude`, and the source checkout's `git status --porcelain` output is byte-identical before and after; its wall time goes into the run record (the 2026-10-01 probe's suite alone took 346 s under load). **Given** a scratch clone in which one of the six files `u1-fork-neutral-tests` fixes is restored to its `88fcfd32` content, **when** the probe runs there, **then** it exits 1 and names the test step, whose tail names that file — the red-first proof that the job goes red on the defects it exists for. **Given** the pull request's CI, **then** `fork probe (this tree as a renamed, suffixed fork)` is green and `all-ci-checks` prints `fork-probe=success`. **Given** the aggregator body rendered with `fork-probe` in {failure, cancelled, skipped} on a full run in `zomarit/stamity`, **then** `bash -e` exits non-zero; with `skipped` in `acme/stamity-private`, **then** it exits 0. |
| `edgeCases` | A pull request from a contributor's fork → runs in `zomarit/stamity` (the base context), with no secret involved, so the job runs. A private import or a GitHub fork's own CI → the job is skipped and the aggregator accepts exactly `skipped` there. The schedule and a manual dispatch → full runs; the job runs in the canonical repository. A records-only change at `88fcfd32` → `fork-probe` is skipped with the heavy lanes. `npx -y npm@11` in a local run without network → the publish steps fail with npm's own error; on CI the bundled npm 11 runs and nothing is fetched. npm.pkg.github.com unreachable during the dry run → the packument read fails quietly and the dry run still exits 0. A timeout-only failure inside the nested suite → `test-run.mjs` re-runs the failed files once and reports `flaky test`; anything else is red. A gate step that rewrites a tracked file → the last step fails naming the paths. `--keep` → the copy stays and its path is printed; without it the copy is removed, after a failure too. |
| `depends_on` | u1-npm-dist-tag (`test/ci/workflow.test.ts`), u1-renovate-suffix (the probe passes `--suffix`), u1-fork-neutral-tests (the suite passes as a fork, `test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts`), u1-git-isolation-headroom (the nested suite's isolation and budgets, `CONTRIBUTING.md`), u1-lane-issues-freshness (`CHANGELOG.md`, its tests then run inside the probe too), u1-secret-shaped-fixtures, u1-lane-config (the probe passes `--lane-config`), u1-lane-package-files (`lockChangesBeyondRoot`), u1-fork-docs-identity (the identity check covers the pages), u1-fork-attestations (`CHANGELOG.md`, last before the probe) |
| `verify` | npx vitest run test/ci/forkProbe.test.ts test/ci/workflow.test.ts test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts test/ci/pluginLocate.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts && npm run lint && npm run typecheck && node scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **files, added:** `test/ci/pluginLocate.test.ts` (one case), `scripts/identity-files.mjs` (imported, not edited).
- **interfaces, added:** Fork step (1) becomes `node scripts/fork-identity.mjs --repository <repository> --registry <registry> --suffix fork --lane-config`, so the copy carries `.stamity/upstream.json` and its nine listed pages carry the probe's identity, both committed by step (2). The gate step `fork-identity.mjs … --suffix fork --check` gains `--lane-config`, so it also verifies the lane file and the pages. `lockfileExtraDiffs` is replaced by `lockChangesBeyondRoot` imported from `scripts/identity-files.mjs` — one definition, shared with the lane. A new gate step `version scheme`, in process, over the copy (export `versionSchemeChecks(copyRoot) → { name, ok, detail }[]`): (a) `renovate/plugins.json`'s `versioningTemplate`, without its `regex:` prefix, matches `plugins/v<X.Y.Z>-fork.1` and `plugins/v<X.Y.Z>-fork.2`, the second's `(major, minor, patch, build)` sorts above the first's, and it does not match `plugins/v<X.Y.Z>-rc.1`; (b) the companion rule carries `ignoreUnstable: false` and `respectLatest: false`; (c) `nextForkVersion(<X.Y.Z>, <X.Y.Z>-fork.1, "fork")` is `<X.Y.Z>-fork.2` and a new base gives `.1`; the two npm steps already prove the dist-tag rule. (d) The locator: `test/ci/pluginLocate.test.ts` gains a case the nested suite runs — a root declaring `^<the checkout's version>` (from `test/support/identity.ts`) resolves a companion installed at exactly that version — which in the copy is `<X.Y.Z>-fork.1`, so the probe's test step proves the locator accepts the fork's own prerelease.
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** `versionSchemeChecks` over a scratch root holding `tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix: "plugins/v", suffix: "fork" })` and a companion with both flags false, **then** every check is ok; with the canonical strict template, **then** (a) fails naming `versioningTemplate`; with `respectLatest: true`, **then** (b) fails (`test/ci/forkProbe.test.ts`). **Given** a local `node scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs --keep`, **then** it prints `PASS version scheme`, and the kept copy's `.stamity/upstream.json` parses with `parseConfig`. **Given** a scratch clone where one of the nine pages is restored to its canonical text after the fork steps, **then** the probe exits 1 naming the identity check step. **Given** `npx vitest run test/ci/pluginLocate.test.ts`, **then** the new case passes on the canonical tree.
- **edgeCases, added:** `.stamity/upstream.json` in the copy arms nothing there: no gate step runs the lane, and the lane tests build their own fixtures.
- **Replaces:** "`lockfileExtraDiffs(before, after) → string[]`, every `packages` key whose entry differs, ignoring the root identity (`name`, `version`, `packages[""].name`, `packages[""].version`);" → "`lockChangesBeyondRoot(before, after)` imported from `scripts/identity-files.mjs` (`u1-lane-package-files`): every `packages` key whose entry differs, the root identity ignored;", and "`lockfileExtraDiffs` returns `[]` for a root-only change and names the package for a dropped `libc` field" → "`lockChangesBeyondRoot` returns `[]` for a root-only change and names the package for a dropped `libc` field".
