A committed flip of `importChoice[AGENTS.md].mode` from `supplement` to `replace` makes `sync` replace the owner's file (r2 M4b). `init` exits 0 whatever it skipped (`init.ts:1180-1181`); a live `sync` exits 1 on a refused path (`src/cli/commands/sync.ts:166`). The import targets are four fixed paths (`src/cli/commands/init/plan.ts:166`, `:174-179`). **New — the flag.** `--import-config <spec>` is repeatable; a value is a mode (the default for every detected path) or `<path>=<mode>` for one path, `<path>` one of `export const IMPORT_TARGET_PATHS = ["AGENTS.md", "AGENT.md", "CLAUDE.md", ".github/copilot-instructions.md"] as const` (`init/plan.ts`, built from its two tables). Commander's `argParser` folds the values into `ImportFlags = { default: ImportMode \| null; byPath: ReadonlyMap<string, ImportMode> }`; an unknown mode or path, one path twice, or two bare modes throw commander's `InvalidArgumentError` (exit 2) naming the four paths and three modes. Per detected path: its own flag, else the bare flag, else the one interactive answer (asked only when some detected path has no flag), else `DEFAULT_IMPORT_MODE`. A flag naming a target with no file there decides nothing. `--json` `decisions` gains `importDecisions: { path, mode }[]`; `importChoice` keeps the bare (or shared) mode for older readers. Why this spelling: one flag keeps one concept, a default and its exceptions; every existing `--import-config <mode>` line keeps its meaning; `path=mode` mirrors the persisted record. **New — the keys.** `importChoiceSpec(path)` builds four rows appended after `gateSpec("all")` (`config.ts:776-779`): `importChoice.AGENTS.md`, `importChoice.AGENT.md`, `importChoice.CLAUDE.md`, `importChoice..github/copilot-instructions.md` — the manifest field plus the path verbatim, so `config get` reads back exactly what `importChoice` records; `choices: IMPORT_MODES`, `read` = the recorded mode or `null`, `resolve` = the mode or `none`. `set` on a path with no recorded decision → `VALIDATION_ERROR` ``no import decision is recorded for ${path}: it held no file when this setup was made, so the engine writes it as its own`` (next: ``to stop writing it, deselect the client that reads it with config set tools``). **New — `src/cli/engine/importChoice.ts`** (PLAN_MAP `"src/cli/engine/importChoice.ts": { unit: "p20-u1-import-mode-per-file", wave: 13 }`; imports `node:crypto`, `node:fs/promises`, `node:path`, `../../merge/managedBlocks.ts` (`stripManagedBlock`), `../../merge/atomicWrite.ts` (`resolveNonClobberingBakPath`, `isSharedRegularFile`), `../../types/*`): `export function ownerTextOf(content: string, path: string): string` (the file with any managed block removed by `stripManagedBlock`, exact) · `export async function takeImportBackup(rootDir: string, path: string): Promise<{ path: string; sha256: string }>` — writes `ownerTextOf(<file>)` to `<path>.bak`, else `<path>.bak.<8 hex>` (`src/merge/atomicWrite.ts:1471-1478`), `O_EXCL \| O_NOFOLLOW`, verified by size and sha256 like `backupBeforeOverwrite` (`src/merge/safeWrite.ts:1048-1081`); refuses a symbolic or hard link at the source · `export async function restoreImportBackup(rootDir: string, decision: ImportDecision, ledger: readonly LedgerEntry[]): Promise<{ restored: true } \| { restored: false; reason: string }>` — renames the backup over the path when the path is a regular unshared file whose bytes hash to a recorded `contentHash` of the engine's row there and the backup (bounded name, regular, unshared) hashes to `backup.sha256`; reasons ``${path} was edited since the engine replaced it`` / ``${backup} no longer holds the original (sha256 mismatch)`` / ``no backup at ${backup}`` · `export async function proveImportBackup(rootDir: string, decision: ImportDecision, ledger: readonly LedgerEntry[]): Promise<boolean>` — the target hashes to a recorded engine `contentHash` (steady state, every ≤1.11.0 `replace` included), or the backup hashes to `backup.sha256` and equals `ownerTextOf(target)` · `export function chartersMissing(manifest: SetupManifest): { client: "claude" \| "codex"; path: string }[]` · `export async function setImportChoice(ctx: { rootDir: string; manifest: SetupManifest; path: string; mode: ImportMode; dryRun: boolean }): Promise<{ manifest: SetupManifest; lines: string[]; backup?: { path: string; sha256: string }; restoredFrom?: string }>`. **What `config set importChoice.<path> <mode>` does** (`applyKeyValue` routes the four keys to `setImportChoice`): `supplement` ↔ `skip` — the manifest only (the next `sync` prepends the block, or strips it exactly through the sweep); to `replace` — `takeImportBackup`, then `{ path, mode: "replace", backup }` and the manifest last (a failed manifest write unlinks the backup it took); from `replace` — `restoreImportBackup`, then `{ path, mode }` (refused with the reason as `VALIDATION_ERROR`, next ``move your edits into ${backup} or delete one of the two, then re-run``, nothing written). `--dry-run` prints `would back up AGENTS.md to AGENTS.md.bak and record it` or `would restore AGENTS.md from AGENTS.md.bak`; JSON adds `backup` and `restoredFrom`. `config` is the only step that moves owner bytes for a decision. **`init`'s `replace`:** after the preflight (`u1-screen-references`) and before the first write, `takeImportBackup` for each `replace` decision, recorded at once; each replaced path is then written whole (`outputWriteOptions(null, …)`, `force: true`, `backup: false`: the import backup is the backup) — so `CLAUDE.md` is replaced like `AGENTS.md` and nothing is screened. This replaces `u1-import-config-round-trip`'s "after the write loop, each replace decision whose result carries `backupPath` gets `backup`". **`sync`'s `replace`:** `planSync` collects `replacePaths` (decisions with `mode: "replace"`); `planOutputEntries` predicts them on the whole-file lane with `force` (an `update`, not an `unmanaged-name` collision); `applySync` writes each with `force: true, backup: false` only when `proveImportBackup` holds, else the path is `skipped` with ``${path} is set to replace, but no recorded backup holds its owner text, so it was not overwritten — run config set importChoice.${path} replace`` and the run exits 1. `sync` never takes or restores an import backup. **No charter:** Claude Code selected and `AGENTS.md` or `CLAUDE.md` decided `skip`, or Codex selected and `AGENTS.md` decided `skip`, prints ``${path} is set to skip, so ${client} reads no charter from this setup: its invariants, verification gates and touchpoints do not reach it${path === "CLAUDE.md" ? " unless your own CLAUDE.md imports AGENTS.md" : ""}. supplement keeps your file and adds the charter in a managed block.`` from `init` (panel warnings), `sync` (report warnings) and `config set` (after the change); warning code `NO_CHARTER`, severity `warn`, once file 02's output contract lands. **`--strict`:** `init --strict` exits 1, after writing the rest as `sync` does, when any planned path was skipped (`wrote[].action === "skipped"`); the default stays 0; `--json` gains `skipped: { path: string; reason: string }[]` (repository-relative POSIX, plan order) on every run; with `--dry-run` it exits 1 when the preview skips. **Panel:** `importNote` reads the recorded decision: a `replace` with a backup → ``existing config: ${path} was replaced — your file is at ${backup.path} (verified)``; a refused write keeps its outcome token; no `.bak` is claimed without a record. **Validation:** unchanged from `u1-import-config-round-trip` (a `backup` only on `replace`, bounded name); a ≤1.11.0 `replace` without `backup` still validates and `sync` proves it by the target's hash. **Follows:** `src/cli/commands/config.ts:543-551` (`gateSpec`: one function, one row per member) and `src/merge/safeWrite.ts:1048-1081` (a verified, non-clobbering backup taken before the original changes). **Backward compatibility:** no new manifest field; every old command line keeps its meaning; a 1.11.0 CLI refuses a manifest carrying `backup` (that unit's named skew). **Coverage floor:** none. |

**when** `config set importChoice.AGENTS.md supplement` then `sync -y`, **then** the block is back (`test/cli/commands/config.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts`). **Given** a supplemented `AGENTS.md`, **when** `config set importChoice.AGENTS.md replace`, **then** `AGENTS.md.bak` equals the owner's original byte for byte, the manifest records it, and `AGENTS.md` is untouched; **when** `sync -y`, **then** `AGENTS.md` is the charter whole; **when** `clean -y`, **then** `AGENTS.md` is the original and no `AGENTS.md.bak*` exists (`test/cli/commands/roundTrip.test.ts`). **Given** a replaced `AGENTS.md`, **when** `config set importChoice.AGENTS.md supplement`, **then** `AGENTS.md` is the original, the backup is gone, the decision has no `backup`, and the next `sync -y` prepends the block; ### u1-ignored-output — setup says when git ignores a file it writes, and `check` fails on it

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u1-ignored-output |
| `requirements` | REQ-FLOW-044 |
| `files` | `src/cli/engine/gitStatus.ts` (new `readIgnoredPaths` beside `readWorkingTreeStatus` `:49-55`), `src/cli/commands/init/apply.ts` (`InitApplyReport` `:132-172`), `src/cli/commands/init/panel.ts` (`renderInitPanel` `:773`), `src/cli/commands/init.ts` (`renderDryRun` `:758`), `src/cli/commands/sync/engine.ts` (`SyncPlan` `:154-191`; `planSync` `:437-530`), `src/cli/commands/sync/report.ts` (`syncJsonPayload` `:314`), `src/cli/commands/check.ts` (header `:65`; `runDoctor` `:1407-1468`; `renderDrift` `:1766-1806`; `renderNextSteps` `:1921-2004`), `docs/troubleshooting.md` (the doctor table; `:83`, `:87-88`; the page header re-verified, since its own re-open rule fires on an added probe), `docs/getting-started.md` (`:286`), `test/cli/engine/gitStatus.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/initApply.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/init.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`, `CHANGELOG.md` (`### Added`) |
| `interfaces` | **Current:** no verb asks git whether a path it writes is ignored; the only `check-ignore` call is the worktree lane's, without `-v`, so it cannot name the rule (`src/worktree/git.ts:425-458`). r1 N8: with `.cursor/` ignored, `init` and `sync` exit 0 in silence; a fresh clone's `check` says "25 file(s) would change, 25 ledgered file(s) missing" and names `sync`, which writes the files where git ignores them again; with `.claude/` ignored, 59 are missing and `.claude/settings.json` (guards, permissions) never reaches a commit. `check` runs fifteen probes (`src/cli/commands/check.ts:1407-1468`); `git-available` warns when git does not answer (`:195-215`). **New (`src/cli/engine/gitStatus.ts`):** `export interface IgnoredPath { readonly path: string; readonly source: string; readonly line: number; readonly pattern: string }` · `export type GitStdinRunner = (args: readonly string[], cwd: string, input: string) => { status: number \| null; stdout: string }` · `export function readIgnoredPaths(cwd: string, relPaths: readonly string[], runner: GitStdinRunner = spawnGit): IgnoredPath[] \| null` — runs `git check-ignore -v -z --stdin` in `cwd` with the NUL-joined paths (measured 2026-10-06 in the planning lab (`gi/`): records are `<source>\0<line>\0<pattern>\0<path>\0`, exit 0 when a path matches and 1 when none does, tracked paths are not reported, a path that does not exist yet is reported); status 0 → the records in input order, 1 → `[]`, any other status, a spawn error or a timeout (`GIT_FACT_TIMEOUT_MS`, `:31`) → `null`; empty `relPaths` → `[]` with no spawn. `spawnGit` = `spawnSync("git", args, { cwd, input, encoding: "utf8", timeout })`. **Init and sync:** after the plan, `readIgnoredPaths(rootDir, outputs.map((o) => o.path))`; `InitApplyReport.ignored: IgnoredPath[] \| null` and `SyncPlan.ignored?: IgnoredPath[] \| null` (`null` = git did not answer; optional in the type as `warnings` is); both runs, dry runs included, warn once per (source, line, pattern): ``warning: ${source}:${line} (${pattern}) ignores ${n} path(s) this setup writes — ${first three}${n > 3 ? ` (+${n - 3} more)` : ""} — so they never reach a commit, and check in a fresh clone reports them missing. Remove or narrow that line, or add !<path> lines after it.``; their JSON carries `ignored`. Nothing is refused: the files are written. **Check:** new probe `checkIgnoredOutput(rootDir, manifest, ignored)` with `const id = "ignored-output"` (the pin `test/docsPages.test.ts:2755-2785` reads probe ids by that pattern), placed after `git-available`; `check`'s `run` calls `readIgnoredPaths` once over every distinct ledger path plus `.stamity/manifest.json` and hands the result to the probe, the drift lines and the next steps. No manifest → `pass` ``nothing ledgered to check``; `null` → `warn` ``skipped — git did not answer here (no binary on PATH, or this directory is not a repository), so no ledgered path was checked against the ignore rules``; `[]` → `pass` ``none of the ${n} ledgered path(s) is ignored by git``; otherwise `fail` ``${n} ledgered path(s) are ignored by git and never reach a commit: ${first three} (${source}:${line} ${pattern})… — a fresh clone's check reports them missing, and sync cannot fix that: it writes them where git ignores them again``. A missing path git ignores prints `missing (ignored: ${source}:${line})`; when the probe fails, `next:` leads with ``edit ${source} line ${line} (${pattern}) so git keeps the paths this setup writes, then commit them — sync alone writes them back where git ignores them``, and the `sync` step stays only for drift on paths git does not ignore. Count words move: `check.ts:65` "fifteen" → "sixteen", `docs/troubleshooting.md:83` "Fifteen probes" → "Sixteen", `:87` "Only seven rows can fail" → "eight" with `ignored-output` listed, `docs/getting-started.md:286` "fifteen" → "sixteen" (re-counted at intake: file 0 or file 02 may add rows first). **Follows:** `src/worktree/git.ts:425-458` (one batched `check-ignore --stdin -z`; 1 means none) and `src/cli/engine/gitStatus.ts:49-55` (a git that does not answer is "unknown", never a throw). **Backward compatibility:** no manifest field; a repository with nothing ignored prints what it did plus the new `pass` row. **Coverage floor:** none. |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** a runner answering status 0 with the four NUL-terminated fields `.gitignore`, `3`, `.cursor/` and `.cursor/a.mdc`, **then** `readIgnoredPaths` returns `[{ path: ".cursor/a.mdc", source: ".gitignore", line: 3, pattern: ".cursor/" }]`; status 1 → `[]`; status 128 or a spawn error → `null` (`test/cli/engine/gitStatus.test.ts`). **Given** a fixture whose `.gitignore` line 3 is `.cursor/`, **when** `init -y --tools claude,cursor`, **then** exit 0, the files are written, one warning names `.gitignore:3 (.cursor/)`, and `--json` `report.ignored` lists every planned `.cursor/` path with `source: ".gitignore"`, `line: 3` (`test/cli/commands/initApply.test.ts`); **when** `sync -y` after a commit, **then** the same warning and JSON `ignored` (`test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts`). **Given** that repository after `git clean -fdX`, **when** `check --json`, **then** exit 1, the `ignored-output` row is `fail` naming `.cursor/hooks.json` and `.gitignore:3`, the first `next` step is the `.gitignore` edit, and no step offers `sync` as the fix for those paths (r1 n8c); **given** `.claude/` ignored instead, **then** the row names `.claude/settings.json` (r1 n8a) (`test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`). **Given** a ledgered path that matches a rule but is tracked (`git add -f`), **then** the row passes. **Given** `.stamity/` ignored, **then** the row names `.stamity/manifest.json`. **Given** git absent (an injected failing runner), **then** the row is `warn` beginning `skipped —`, and `init` and `sync` print no ignore warning. `test/docsPages.test.ts` passes with the new troubleshooting row. **Re-pin** `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts:478` ("returns the fifteen doctor rows in a fixed order") to sixteen with `ignored-output` after `git-available`, with `// TEST CHANGE, justified: REQ-FLOW-044 — a sixteenth probe`. |
| `edgeCases` | A rule in `.git/info/exclude` or a global excludes file → `source` names that file, and the warning reads the same (the fix is outside the repository's `.gitignore`). A negation (`!.cursor/hooks.json`) after `.cursor/` → not ignored, not reported. A nested `.cursor/.gitignore` → `source: ".cursor/.gitignore"`. `.stamity/backups/` (self-ignored, REQ-FLOW-046) and the review gate's state are not ledger rows and are never checked. Thousands of paths → one process over stdin. `sync --dry-run` → warns as the live run (a planning fact). |
| `depends_on` | u1-copilot-setup-steps (one writer at a time on `src/cli/commands/sync/engine.ts`) |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/cli/engine/gitStatus.test.ts test/cli/commands/initApply.test.ts test/cli/commands/init.test.ts test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts test/cli/commands/check.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts` · `npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test` |

### u1-drift-cause — `check` tells a needed re-sync from tampering, and exits 3 when only a re-sync is needed

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u1-drift-cause |
| `requirements` | REQ-FLOW-042, REQ-FLOW-043 |
| `files` | `src/cli/commands/check.ts` (`DriftReport` `:119-128`; `runDriftGate` `:1490-1510`; `renderDrift` `:1766-1806`; `renderNextSteps` `:1921-2004`; the command `:2082-2167`, `ok` `:2108-2111`, exit `:2130`, drift JSON `:2133-2141`; `checkFailureDoc` `:2170-2205`), `src/cli/kit/program.ts` (header `:29-40`; `CommandResult` `:107-110`; `runCli` `:193`, `:270`, `:351-363`), `src/cli/kit/output.ts` (`FailureDoc.code` `:15`), `src/cli/docs/cliReference.ts` (`EXIT_STATUSES` `:102-106`; the section `:357-379`), `src/merge/managedBlocks.ts` (new `managedSpanAsEmitted`), `docs/cli-reference.md` (regenerated), `docs/troubleshooting.md` (exit table `:62-76`; new `### Drift a re-sync resolves (exit 3)` after `:128-148`; the page header re-verified, since its own re-open rule fires on a new exit status), `docs/getting-started.md` (`:288-292`), `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`, `test/merge/managedBlocks.test.ts`, `test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts` (re-pin `:364-367`), `test/cli/kit.test.ts`, `test/detect/repoAnalyzer.test.ts`, `CHANGELOG.md` (`### Added`, `### Changed`) |
| `interfaces` | **Current:** `check` re-plans with fresh detection (`runDriftGate` → `planSync`, `src/cli/commands/check.ts:1490-1491`; `analyzeRepo`, `src/cli/commands/sync/engine.ts:455`), so an owner's change to a detection input, or to owner text the emission repeats, is drift, and every drift exits 1 with `INTEGRITY_ERROR` (`check.ts:2130`, `:2197`). r2 M11 measured each of these failing `check` alone: a new `lint` script (17 files), a lockfile, a new owner workflow, a Dependabot bump of `actions/checkout` in the owner's `ci.yml`, a new `.nvmrc`, and with Codex an owner edit outside `AGENTS.md`'s block (`AGENTS.override.md`, `src/adapters/codex.ts:556`). The exit contract is 0/1/2 (`src/cli/kit/program.ts:29-35`, `:108`, `:193`; `EXIT_STATUSES`, `src/cli/docs/cliReference.ts:102-106`, pinned at `test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts:364-367`). **New — classification (`check.ts`):** `export type DriftCause = "resync" \| "tamper"` · `export type DriftReason = "inputs-moved" \| "new-output" \| "edited" \| "missing" \| "ignored" \| "collision" \| "pending-delete" \| "unproven"` · `export interface DriftRow { readonly path: string; readonly kind: "change" \| "missing" \| "reclaim"; readonly action?: SyncPlanEntry["action"]; readonly cause: DriftCause; readonly reason: DriftReason }`; `DriftReport` gains `rows: DriftRow[]` and `cause: DriftCause \| null` (`null` when clean, `resync` only when every row is). `runDriftGate(rootDir, engineVersion, opts)` (the `opts` of `u1-fork-neutral-tests`) gains `opts.ignored?: ReadonlySet<string>` (from `u1-ignored-output`) and classifies, first match wins: an `update` whose engine-written bytes on disk are intact → `resync/inputs-moved`, otherwise `tamper/edited`, where intact means a merged MCP document (`MERGED_MCP_JSON_PATHS`) hashes whole to a recorded `contentHash`, `.claude/settings.json` proves every engine member against the rows' `coOwned.members` (file 0's `proveMember`) and without a member record hashes whole, an output with a managed block has `sha256(managedSpanAsEmitted(file, path))` equal to a recorded hash, and any other output hashes whole to one; a path whose rows carry no hash → `tamper/unproven`; a `create` at a path no ledger row names → `resync/new-output`, at a ledgered path → `tamper/ignored` when git ignores it, else `tamper/missing`; a `collision` → `tamper/collision`; each `missing` path → `tamper/ignored` or `tamper/missing`; each reclaim candidate → `tamper/pending-delete` (a deletion never rides an automated re-sync). **`src/merge/managedBlocks.ts`:** `export function managedSpanAsEmitted(content: string, filePath?: string): string \| null` — the on-disk BEGIN…END lines with `\n` terminators and one final `\n`, the shape `wrapInManagedBlock` returns (`:387-391`), which is what the ledger hashes for a managed output (`src/cli/engine/emissionWrite.ts:200-219`); `null` with no block. **New — the exit:** `check` exits 0 when green, **3** when no doctor row fails, drift was evaluated and `drift.cause === "resync"`, else 1. `CommandResult.exitCode: 0 \| 1 \| 3`; `runCli` "Returns 0 \| 1 \| 2 \| 3 only." and treats 3 like 1 for the JSON document (`ok: false`, `:351-363`); the header's exit contract adds "3 re-sync needed, from `check` only". `FailureDoc.code` gains the CLI-edge code `"RESYNC_NEEDED"` (`src/cli/kit/output.ts:15`); exit 3's `error` = `{ code: "RESYNC_NEEDED", message: "check found drift that a re-sync resolves: the repository's own inputs moved, and nothing the engine wrote was edited", why: "<n> file(s) differ: <first three paths>", next: packageCommand("sync") }`. `EXIT_STATUSES` gains `{ status: 3, meaning: "check only: re-sync needed — the files differ from the engine's output only because the repository's own inputs moved; run the pinned sync. Never a pass." }`; the page reads "Four, and only four." and its CLI-edge sentence names `RESYNC_NEEDED` beside `USAGE` and `FAILURE`. No other verb returns 3. **New — output:** each drift line gains its cause (`  update    AGENTS.override.md  (re-sync: inputs moved)`, `  update    .claude/agents/stamity-creator.md  (edited)`); the summary ends ``— all of it re-sync: the repository's own inputs moved`` or ``— ${m} row(s) are not explained by moved inputs``; when `cause === "resync"`, `next:` is ``${packageCommand("sync")} — re-sync needed (exit 3): ${n} file(s) differ only because detection inputs or owner text moved``; JSON `drift` gains `rows` and `cause`, and `changes`, `missing`, `reclaimPending` keep their shape. **Detection inputs (REQ-FLOW-043), answered for the brief's question:** the analyzer's probes (`src/detect/repoAnalyzer.ts:407-437`) read the repository root's own files and entries — the language indicators (`:44-63`), root `*.csproj`/`*.sln` (`:521`), root TypeScript extensions (`:531-572`), `package.json` and the lockfiles, `pnpm-workspace.yaml`, `lerna.json`, `pyproject.toml`, `Dockerfile`, `data/` and root data files (`:778-792`), and the agent-tool folders by existence — plus the names of the files in `.github/workflows/` (`:756-772`) and the `package.json` of each monorepo package the root's workspace configuration names (`:596-625`, `:905-930`); the Copilot setup workflow reads `.nvmrc`, `.node-version` and `engines.node` (`src/adapters/copilot.ts:685-709`). Owner text emission repeats: `AGENTS.md` outside the block (Codex's override), `.stamity/overrides/**`, `.stamity/hooks/**`, installed packs. Is reading the owner's CI workflow needed? Its presence, yes: the charter states the CI provider, and the first workflow flips it once (`resync`). Its `uses:` lines, no: `u1-copilot-setup-steps` (S8 revised) stops copying action refs, so a Dependabot or Renovate bump in the owner's workflows is no longer drift at all. No other folder is read, so a pin folder such as `.tools/stamity/` changes no generated byte unless the root's workspace configuration names it (r2 M14). `docs/troubleshooting.md` gains `### Drift a re-sync resolves (exit 3)`: the inputs, the two causes, and "never merge on exit 3 without running the pinned sync". **Follows:** `src/cli/commands/check.ts:2108-2111` (one value read by the exit code, the close and the payload) and `src/merge/reclaim.ts` gate 2b (a recorded hash is the authorship proof). **Backward compatibility:** additive JSON; exit 3 replaces 1 only for runs that are re-sync only — a consumer branching on non-zero is unchanged, one branching on `== 1` sees 3 (CHANGELOG `### Changed`). **Coverage floor:** `src/merge/managedBlocks.ts` 99/98/100/100 (`vitest.config.ts:187`). |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** a committed `--tools claude,copilot` Node fixture, **when** a `"lint"` script is added and `check --json` runs, **then** exit 3, `drift.cause === "resync"`, every row `inputs-moved`, `error.code === "RESYNC_NEEDED"`, and `error.next` is the pinned `sync`; **when** `sync -y` then `check`, **then** exit 0 (`test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`). **Given** each of a new `package-lock.json`, a first owner workflow `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, a new `.nvmrc`, and Codex selected with an owner line added outside `AGENTS.md`'s block, **then** exit 3 (r2 M11). **Given** a bump of `actions/checkout` in the owner's `ci.yml` after `sync`, **then** exit 0 (no drift). **Given** an edit inside `.claude/agents/stamity-creator.md`, **then** exit 1, its row `tamper/edited`, `error.code === "INTEGRITY_ERROR"`; **given** both that edit and a new script, **then** exit 1 with both causes in `drift.rows`. **Given** a ledgered file deleted, **then** exit 1 `missing`; **given** a client deselected with `config set tools` and no sync, **then** exit 1 with `pending-delete` rows; **given** a collision, **then** exit 1 `collision`; **given** a doctor `fail` beside re-sync-only drift, **then** exit 1. **Given** a supplemented `AGENTS.md` whose block is intact while the charter moved, **then** `inputs-moved`; **given** its block body edited, **then** `edited`. **Given** every managed output of the golden repository prepended over owner text, **then** `managedSpanAsEmitted(file, path) === output.content` (`test/merge/managedBlocks.test.ts`). **Given** `EXIT_STATUSES`, **then** statuses `[0, 1, 2, 3]` and `src/cli/kit/program.ts` contains `Returns 0 \| 1 \| 2 \| 3 only` (re-pin `test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts:364-367` with `// TEST CHANGE, justified: REQ-FLOW-042 — check exits 3 when only a re-sync is needed`); `runCli` returns 3 for a command returning 3 and prints one `ok: false` document (`test/cli/kit.test.ts`). **Given** a Node fixture and a copy with `.tools/stamity/{package.json (a lint script), package-lock.json, .npmrc}` committed, **then** `analyzeRepo` returns equal summaries and every planned output is byte-identical (r2 M14) (`test/detect/repoAnalyzer.test.ts`). |
| `edgeCases` | A committed `contentHash` forged to match an edited engine file → `resync`; harmless, because exit 3 is never a pass and its remedy rewrites the file from the engine (the guide: never merge on 3 without the pinned sync). A pin moved without regeneration (`generatedBy` older than the running CLI) → engine bytes intact → `resync` (an unapproved version is file 0's `check --expect-version`). `drift: not evaluated` (the plan threw) → exit 1, unchanged. A pre-1.1.0 ledger row with no hash → `tamper/unproven` until a sync records hashes. An MCP document with a hand-added server and a moved engine server → `tamper/edited` (whole-file proof; follow-up row). A pending strip after `config set importChoice.<path> skip` → `pending-delete`, exit 1, remedy `sync` (a person reviews it). `workspace sync` reads no `check` status → unchanged. |
| `depends_on` | u1-init-force-charter (lane order), u1-ignored-output (`opts.ignored`, `src/cli/commands/check.ts`), docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-00.md (`u0-check-expectations` edits `check.ts` first, `proveMember`) |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/cli/commands/check.test.ts test/merge/managedBlocks.test.ts test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts test/cli/kit.test.ts test/detect/repoAnalyzer.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts` · `node scripts/generate-docs.mjs --page cli` · `npm test -- --coverage` · `npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test` |

### u1-repo-root — every verb finds the setup from any folder inside the repository, and `init` refuses a second setup below one

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u1-repo-root |
| `requirements` | REQ-FLOW-045 |
| `files` | `src/cli/engine/setupRoot.ts` (new), `test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts` (one PLAN_MAP row), `src/cli/commands/learn.ts` (`requireStateDir` `:163-175`; `run` `:443-447`), `src/cli/commands/handoff.ts` (`requireStateDir` `:139-150`; `:260`, `:358`, `:417`, `:527`, `:591`, `:656`, `:702`), `src/cli/commands/sync.ts` (`:141`), `src/cli/commands/check.ts` (`:2090`), `src/cli/commands/clean.ts` (`:637`), `src/cli/commands/config.ts` (`:1280`), `src/cli/commands/add.ts` (`:723`), `src/cli/commands/validate.ts` (`:1366`), `src/cli/commands/plugin.ts` (`status` `:348`; `setup` `:361`), `src/cli/commands/ledger.ts` (`:256`, `:389`, `:607`), `src/cli/commands/init.ts` (`:926`, ahead of `:950`), `test/cli/engine/setupRoot.test.ts` (new), `test/cli/commands/repoRoot.test.ts` (new), `test/cli/commands/learn.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/handoff.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/init.test.ts`, `CHANGELOG.md` (`### Fixed`) |
| `interfaces` | **Current:** every verb takes the working directory as the repository root (`const rootDir = ctx.app.runtime.cwd` at the sites above). r3 C9.2, measured from `src/deep` of an initialised repository: `learn capture` and `handoff prepare` exit 1 "this repo is not initialised" with `next: run: npx -y @zomarit/stamity@1.11.0 init` (`learn.ts:163-175`, `handoff.ts:139-150`); following it, `init -y` exits 0 and writes a second setup under `src/deep/`; `handoff list` and `prune` exit 0 with zero for `src/deep/.stamity/handoffs`. The workspace verbs already walk up for `workspace.json` (`detectWorkspaceContext`, `src/workspace/detect.ts:164-183`; `ancestorChain` `:189-199`). **New `src/cli/engine/setupRoot.ts`** (PLAN_MAP `"src/cli/engine/setupRoot.ts": { unit: "p20-u1-repo-root", wave: 13 }`; imports `node:child_process`, `node:fs/promises`, `node:path`, `../../types/manifest.ts` (`MANIFEST_FILE`), `../../types/markers.ts` (`STATE_DIR`) and type `GitRunner` from `../../workspace/git.ts`): `export interface SetupRoot { readonly rootDir: string; readonly walkedFrom: string \| null; readonly gitTop: string \| null }` · `export async function resolveSetupRoot(cwd: string, runner?: GitRunner): Promise<SetupRoot>` — `gitTop` = `git rev-parse --show-toplevel` in `cwd` (5 s; `null` when git does not answer); no `gitTop` → `{ rootDir: cwd, walkedFrom: null, gitTop: null }`; else `realpath(cwd)` and each ancestor up to and including `gitTop`, nearest first, and the first holding `.stamity/manifest.json` (`lstat`, any entry) is `rootDir`, with `walkedFrom: cwd` when it differs; none → `rootDir: cwd` · `export async function findGoverningSetup(cwd: string, runner?: GitRunner): Promise<string \| null>` — the same walk from the parent of `cwd`. **Verbs:** `sync`, `check`, `clean`, `config`, `add`, `validate`, `plugin status`, `learn`, every `handoff` mode and `ledger` take `rootDir` from `resolveSetupRoot(ctx.app.runtime.cwd)`; when `walkedFrom !== null` they print one stderr line ``using the setup at ${relative(cwd, rootDir) \|\| "."}`` (stderr, so `--json` keeps one stdout document). `--body-file` stays relative to the working directory (`learn.ts:186`, `handoff.ts:157`). `learn`/`handoff` with no setup up to `gitTop`: message ``no setup governs this folder: no ${STATE_DIR}/manifest.json here or above it up to ${gitTop}``, `next` ``run stamity init at the repository root (${gitTop}), not in this folder`` (no `gitTop`: today's text); `learn` keeps its `.stamity/`-exists precondition on the root it found. **`init` and `plugin setup`:** `findGoverningSetup(cwd)` non-null → `VALIDATION_ERROR` ``a setup already governs this folder: ${relative(cwd, governing)}/${STATE_DIR}/manifest.json``, `why` ``every verb run here finds that setup; a second one below it splits the ledger and the state``, `next` ``run the verb you meant from here — it uses that setup; to give this folder a setup of its own anyway, re-run init with --force``; `init --force` proceeds (the `workspace init` nesting precedent, `docs/workspaces.md:445`), `plugin setup` has no override. `worktree` and `workspace` keep their own root rules. **Follows:** `src/workspace/detect.ts:164-199` (a nearest-first ancestor walk) and `src/cli/engine/gitStatus.ts:49-55` (git not answering is an answer). **Backward compatibility:** from the root nothing changes; no manifest field. **Coverage floor:** none. |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** an initialised repository with an empty `src/deep/`, **when** `learn capture --title t --summary s` runs there with a body on stdin, **then** exit 0, the learning lands in `<root>/.stamity/learnings/` and stderr says `using the setup at ../..`; **when** `handoff prepare`, `list`, `resume`, `complete` and `prune` run there, **then** each acts on `<root>/.stamity/handoffs/` (r3 C9.2) (`test/cli/commands/learn.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/handoff.test.ts`). **When** `init -y` runs in `src/deep/`, **then** exit 1 naming `../../.stamity/manifest.json` and nothing appears under `src/deep/`; **when** `init -y --force`, **then** a nested setup is created (`test/cli/commands/init.test.ts`). **When** `sync`, `check`, `config get tools`, `validate`, `clean --dry-run`, `plugin status` and `ledger` run in `src/deep/`, **then** each reports on the root setup (`test/cli/commands/repoRoot.test.ts`, new, through `runCli` with `cwd`). **Given** a nested git repository `vendor/lib/` with its own `.git`, **when** `learn capture` runs in `vendor/lib/src`, **then** exit 1 naming `vendor/lib` as where `init` belongs. **Given** a folder with no git, **then** every verb uses the working directory. **Given** a member folder holding its own `.stamity/manifest.json` below an initialised root, **then** verbs in it use the member's setup. **Given** `resolveSetupRoot` with a runner that throws, **then** `{ rootDir: cwd, walkedFrom: null, gitTop: null }`; **given** a symlinked `cwd`, **then** the walk runs from its real path (`test/cli/engine/setupRoot.test.ts`). **Given** `learn capture --body-file notes.md` in `src/deep/`, **then** `src/deep/notes.md` is read. |
| `edgeCases` | `clean -y` in a subfolder removes the root setup: the stderr line names it first, and the interactive confirmation names the root's count. `.stamity/` at the root with `manifest.json` gone → the walk finds nothing; `learn` from the root still captures (cwd is the root), from a subfolder it refuses naming the git root. A `cwd` reached through a symlink outside `gitTop` → `realpath` first; still outside → no walk. A deep folder → bounded by `gitTop`, not by a count. Windows → `rev-parse` prints forward slashes, normalised with `path.resolve`. A worktree lane checkout → its own git top, its own setup. |
| `depends_on` | u1-drift-cause (one writer at a time on `src/cli/commands/check.ts`, the last of the command edits) |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/cli/engine/setupRoot.test.ts test/cli/commands/repoRoot.test.ts test/cli/commands/learn.test.ts test/cli/commands/handoff.test.ts test/cli/commands/init.test.ts test/cli/commands/sync.test.ts test/cli/commands/check.test.ts test/cli/commands/clean.test.ts test/cli/commands/config.test.ts test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts` · `npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test` · `npm run knip` |

## Execution order

*Rewritten 2026-10-06 for the updated brief:* `u1-settings-key-ownership` moved to file 0, and this file gained six fork
units and five engine units. It now runs as **two sessions, one pull request each**, in the order below.

1. **Intake, once per session** (the freshness guard): file 0 (`docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-00.md`) and Packages
   19, 18, 22 and Track B merged, and every anchor this file cites re-read against the landed tree. Record in the run
   record the names file 0 landed that these units build on: the co-owned-document API and `LedgerEntry.coOwned`
   (`u0-settings-ownership`), the owned-path bound module and its import-decision rules (`u0-ledger-bound`), the
   registry-bound call form (`u0-registry-bound-calls`) and the `check --expect-*` flags (`u0-check-expectations`).
   Where Package 18 changed a shape a unit depends on (the CI aggregator and its `records` job, the lane's fetch, the
   import recipe, `vitest.config.ts`, CONTRIBUTING and GOVERNANCE), the unit works from the landed text;
   `u1-fork-ci-job` states both shapes. Track B's close writes this package's kickoff, which also carries Package 22's
   pack amendments (a made-up fork pack in the fork CI job, the intake reproduction on that pack, `upgrade` re-adding
   installed packs, and one route-page line).
2. **Session 1 — the fork (lanes A and A2), one pull request:**
   - **Lane A (fork, tests and the lane), serial:** `u1-npm-dist-tag` → `u1-fork-neutral-tests` →
     `u1-secret-shaped-fixtures` → `u1-renovate-suffix` → `u1-git-isolation-headroom` → `u1-lane-package-files` →
     `u1-lane-config` → `u1-lane-issues-freshness` → `u1-lane-app-token` → `u1-fork-docs-identity`.
   - **Lane A2 (the fork release), its own worktree** once `u1-npm-dist-tag` and `u1-secret-shaped-fixtures` have merged
     into the session branch: `u1-fork-release-hardening` → `u1-fork-attestations`. It shares only
     `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (other sections), `test/docsPages.test.ts` and `CHANGELOG.md` with lane A.
3. **Session 2 — the round trip and the engine (lane B), one pull request, then the fork CI job:**
   - **Lane B, serial, one writer at a time:** `u1-gitignore-lines` → `u1-import-config-round-trip` →
     `u1-clean-keeps-state` → `u1-screen-references` → `u1-foreign-paths` → `u1-import-mode-per-file` →
     `u1-copilot-setup-steps` → `u1-ignored-output` → `u1-manifest-stable` → `u1-init-force-charter` → `u1-drift-cause`
     → `u1-repo-root` → `u1-node-missing-hint`. A split point inside the session, if one is needed: B1 =
     `u1-gitignore-lines` … `u1-import-mode-per-file`; B2 = `u1-copilot-setup-steps` … `u1-node-missing-hint`, after B1
     has merged into the session branch.
   - **Last: `u1-fork-ci-job`**, after lanes A, A2 and B, because its probe gates the whole file's result as a fork.
4. **One valid serial order**, for a run that keeps one lane: `u1-npm-dist-tag`, `u1-fork-neutral-tests`,
   `u1-secret-shaped-fixtures`, `u1-renovate-suffix`, `u1-git-isolation-headroom`, `u1-lane-package-files`,
   `u1-lane-config`, `u1-lane-issues-freshness`, `u1-lane-app-token`, `u1-fork-docs-identity`,
   `u1-fork-release-hardening`, `u1-fork-attestations`, `u1-gitignore-lines`, `u1-import-config-round-trip`,
   `u1-clean-keeps-state`, `u1-screen-references`, `u1-foreign-paths`, `u1-import-mode-per-file`,
   `u1-copilot-setup-steps`, `u1-ignored-output`, `u1-manifest-stable`, `u1-init-force-charter`, `u1-drift-cause`,
   `u1-repo-root`, `u1-node-missing-hint`, `u1-fork-ci-job`.
5. **Shared files have one writer at a time**, through the session's integrator in integration order:
   - `CHANGELOG.md` (each unit's bullet is fixed in its `interfaces`; `## [Unreleased]` exists once file 0 has merged),
     `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (lanes A and A2), `docs/plugins.md`, `docs/troubleshooting.md` and
     `docs/getting-started.md` (lane B; `u1-fork-docs-identity` edits `docs/troubleshooting.md` only after lane B's units
     that touch it, so in session 2's order it re-reads the landed page), `test/docsPages.test.ts`
     (`REATTESTATION_DATE` and the hand-page headers), `CONTRIBUTING.md` (`u1-git-isolation-headroom`, then
     `u1-fork-ci-job`).
   - `scripts/fork-identity.mjs` (`u1-renovate-suffix` → `u1-lane-package-files` → `u1-lane-config` →
     `u1-fork-docs-identity`); `scripts/upstream.mjs` (`u1-secret-shaped-fixtures` → `u1-lane-package-files` →
     `u1-lane-issues-freshness` → `u1-fork-docs-identity`); `.github/workflows/upstream-update.yml`
     (`u1-lane-issues-freshness` → `u1-lane-app-token`); `.github/workflows/fork-release.yml` (`u1-npm-dist-tag` →
     `u1-fork-release-hardening` → `u1-fork-attestations`); `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts`
     (`u1-fork-neutral-tests` → `u1-secret-shaped-fixtures` → `u1-renovate-suffix` → `u1-lane-package-files` →
     `u1-lane-config` → `u1-fork-docs-identity`); `test/upstream/lane.test.ts` (`u1-secret-shaped-fixtures` →
     `u1-lane-package-files` → `u1-lane-issues-freshness` → `u1-fork-docs-identity`); `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`
     (`u1-npm-dist-tag` → `u1-secret-shaped-fixtures` → `u1-fork-release-hardening` → `u1-fork-attestations`);
     `test/ci/workflow.test.ts` (`u1-npm-dist-tag` → `u1-lane-issues-freshness` → `u1-fork-attestations` →
     `u1-fork-ci-job`); the census at `test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts:209` (`u1-lane-issues-freshness` →
     `u1-fork-ci-job`).
   - `src/cli/commands/check.ts` (`u1-fork-neutral-tests` in session 1; then `u1-screen-references`, `u1-foreign-paths`,
     `u1-ignored-output`, `u1-drift-cause`, `u1-repo-root`); `src/cli/commands/init/apply.ts` and
     `src/cli/commands/sync/engine.ts` (lane B's order, for the units that touch them); `src/cli/commands/init.ts`
     (`u1-screen-references`, `u1-import-mode-per-file`, `u1-ignored-output`, `u1-manifest-stable`,
     `u1-init-force-charter`, `u1-repo-root`); `src/cli/commands/config.ts` (`u1-import-mode-per-file`'s rows, then
     `u1-copilot-setup-steps`' row, appended last so the picker's earlier indexes hold); `src/merge/safeWrite.ts`
     (`u1-import-config-round-trip`, `u1-screen-references`); `src/merge/reclaim.ts` (two units,
     `u1-import-config-round-trip` then `u1-foreign-paths`, under its 100% coverage floor; file 0 changed its co-owned
     drift line first); the cross-client golden snapshot (`u1-copilot-setup-steps`, then `u1-manifest-stable`, then
     `u1-node-missing-hint`, each written with the test file named first and `--update` last).
6. **The eval case that moves:** `u1-clean-keeps-state` rewrites the generated `st-setup` body and moves the adversarial
   case `evals/cases-v6/adversarial/st-setup-refuses-generated-setup.md` (and any case whose `source:` range the edit
   shifts), recorded in `evals/SET-v7.md`; file 3's release run measures it. Every unit that adds an import between
   `src/` modules runs `test/architecture` in its narrow set (learning `a-new-import-runs-the-architecture-test`).
7. **The Prove phase** (`/st-work`) of each session merges the spec-delta entries of the units that session landed.
8. **The gates, per session:** `npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test`, then `npm test -- --coverage` and
   `npm run check`, and `node scripts/ci/fork-probe.mjs` once `u1-fork-ci-job` lands; CI's coverage, Windows and
   fork-probe legs are required. Each pull request title is a conventional-commit subject, for example
   `feat(forks): the lane derives a fork's package files, an App token, opt-in attestations` (session 1) and
   `fix(engine): an exact round trip, a secret screen that reads references, and drift by cause` (session 2).

## Risks

| Risk | Severity | Guard |
|---|---|---|
| The nested full suite in the merge-blocking `fork-probe` job flakes and blocks merges | Warning | `scripts/ci/test-run.mjs` re-runs a timeout-only failure once and reports it as flaky; `u1-git-isolation-headroom` lands first (isolation, budgets); the timeout is derived (20 min, 2.3x the slowest observed) and re-derived from the first ten runs |
| `fork-probe` becomes the longest lane on every pull request (about 6-8.5 min beside a 4-6.5 min check matrix) | Minor | It runs in parallel with `check`, only in the canonical repository, and fetches nothing `check` does not already fetch |
| `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1` changes git on the Windows legs (Git for Windows' system configuration is skipped) | Warning | `fscache = true` in the test-owned file; the windows legs already pin `autocrlf=false` globally; compare the two Windows shards' Test durations with main's last green runs on the pull request |
| The rehearsal job cannot download its artifact under `permissions: {}` | Warning | unverified; the first fork rehearsal dispatch is the probe; the named fallback is `contents: read` (`pack-signing-rehearsal.yml:verify`) with its test pin moved |
| The suffix-aware tag rule's optional group lets Renovate propose upstream `plugins/v*` tags that a fork imported with the old `--mirror` recipe | Warning | `depNameTemplate` is the fork's own repository; Package 18's f1 stops importing those tags; file 03's reset guide deletes them |
| A forged `integrated_tag` from the job that runs the fork's code closes lane issues early | Minor | Shape-checked in `publish` like every other control field; closing is reversible, and each close carries a comment and the run link; it cannot reach any issue without the lane's marker |
| Closing by marker closes a human issue whose first line is the exact marker | Minor | First line only, exact match, open issues only, tags at or below the integrated release; the same identity already lets the lane rewrite such a body |
| A fork's own `tagPattern` or `branch` still fails `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts:282` and gets presets for `plugins/v` | Minor | Follow-up rows; the fork probe and the guide use the default pattern |
| Package 18 lands first and moves the anchors under five of these units (the CI aggregator, the lane script and tests, the fork guide, CONTRIBUTING and GOVERNANCE, `vitest.config.ts`) | Warning | Intake re-research (the freshness guard); `u1-fork-ci-job` states both shapes; every anchor here is `88fcfd32`'s |
| With the test exemptions gone, a future fixture fails the leak gate | Minor | Intended; the guard's message names run-time assembly as the remedy |
| `npx -y npm@11` in a local probe run fetches a floating npm 11 | Minor | CI uses Node 24's bundled npm; the local fallback is the same publisher's npm, pinned to the major whose behaviour is under test |
| `status` writes a file into `.git/` on every online run | Minor | Temporary file and rename, inside the git directory, never the working tree; `--offline` only reads it |
| `src/merge/reclaim.ts` and `src/emit/stateScaffold.ts` sit under the 100% floor; two units add branches to `reclaim.ts` (file 0 changed its co-owned drift line first) | Warning | Each such unit's `verify` runs `npm test -- --coverage`; both arms of each new branch are named in its test criteria |
| `clean -y`'s default narrows (state kept): a script that relied on an empty `.stamity/` afterwards keeps files | Warning | `### Changed` CHANGELOG bullet naming `--purge`; `docs/plugins.md` and the `st-setup` body updated in the same unit |
| A skill folder with an unmanaged pre-existing file is now foreign: `--force` no longer clears it | Warning | Named in the CHANGELOG and the troubleshooting section; the remedy says move the folder aside; see Concerns |
| `MANAGED_ACTION_PINS` must move with every Actions bump of `ci.yml`, or the equality test turns the bump PR red | Warning | `.github/dependabot.yml` comment and the test's message tell the merger to move the table |
| Two lanes of twelve and thirteen units (2026-10-06) | Warning | The file runs as two sessions, one pull request each (Execution order); lane B splits at `u1-copilot-setup-steps` if needed |
| Package 18 rewrites `scripts/upstream.mjs` and lane tests and edits `docs/plugins.md` code spans (`:100`, `:104`, `:564`) before this package | Warning | The freshness guard: re-read `test/upstream/workflowRecovery.test.ts` and every `docs/plugins.md` anchor at intake |
| A 1.11.0 CLI refuses a manifest whose import decision carries `backup` (strict keys) and drops `coOwned` on its own writes | Minor | Mixed-version skew is on the drop list (C4); CHANGELOG notes 1.12.0 manifests need a 1.12.0 CLI |
| A 1.11.0 `check` reads bare managed-block markers as stale | Minor | Same skew note |
| Three units update the cross-client golden snapshot | Minor | Sequenced (6 → 7 → 9); each update reviewed as a diff |
| Windows: CRLF manifests, `.gitignore` and settings; native `backupPath`; POSIX guard tail | Minor | CRLF fold in `writeManifest`; EOL-preserving reducers; POSIX `backup.path`; the PowerShell residual stays documented |
| A vitest call with an optional-value flag ahead of a path (`--json`, `--outputFile`, `-u`, `--update`) writes into a test file: during this plan's drafting on 2026-10-01 a drafter ran `npx vitest list --json test/merge/coverageGaps.test.ts` in the main checkout, and `--json` took the path as its output file and overwrote the test (restored from HEAD the same hour) | Warning | every command in this file names its files before any flag; snapshot updates use `npx vitest run <file> --update`, and a `vitest list` output is named inside the flag, `npx vitest list <file> --json=<out>`, since `list` never reads `--outputFile` (learning `vitest-update-flag-takes-an-optional-value`, widened on 2026-10-08 to `list --json` and `--outputFile`; amended 2026-10-08: it read "to be widened to `--json` and `--outputFile`: a follow-up row") |
| The derivation copies a fork identity that was hand-made rather than written by `fork-identity.mjs` | Minor | The identity gate (`--lane-config --check`) runs on the merged tree and names any difference |
| A fork's own `regenerate` command re-locks with npm 10 after the derivation | Minor | It rewrites a tracked path outside `generatedPaths`, so the run ends `regenerate-failed` naming the lock (`scripts/upstream.mjs:2033-2044`) |
| The App's private key, held as a repository secret, can mint tokens for every repository the App is installed on | Warning | The guide says to install the App on the fork repository only; the workflow narrows each token to this repository and three permissions |
| A scheduled run's empty `github.event.repository` widens an App token to the whole installation | Warning | The repository name comes from `GITHUB_REPOSITORY` and an empty value fails the step before the mint (pinned in the test) |
| `id-token` beside npm in a fork's publish job changes npm 11's behaviour | Warning | No OIDC grant in `publish`; the attestation has its own job |
| A fork on a plan without attestations sets the variable | Minor | The attest job runs after the release exists; the run is red and the release is complete |
| The packages REST endpoint refuses the per-run token, so a public package goes unnoticed | Warning | The repository-visibility refusal comes first; the warning names the setting to confirm; a follow-up records the first real run's answer |
| `tag-moved` turns every scheduled run red until a person acts | Minor | The message prints the exact `acceptedTagMoves` line; `STAMITY_UPSTREAM_SCHEDULE=off` silences the schedule meanwhile |
| A scheduled tick GitHub starts more than an hour late misses its slot | Minor | The next listed hour runs; the heartbeat threshold allows one missed slot |
| Package 21 rewrites the nine pages and introduces a canonical spelling the rewrite does not know | Warning | The new docs case pins the remaining canonical mentions per page and fails on a new one |
| The worker cap slows the maintainer's local suite (7 workers to 4) | Minor | `STAMITY_TEST_MAX_WORKERS` or `--maxWorkers` raises it |
| A future `gh` step in `publish` without its own `GH_TOKEN` runs unauthenticated | Minor | The test asserts every step that runs `gh` carries the token expression |
| The fork release's notes and stamp steps drift from `release.yml`'s copies | Minor | Byte-equality cases for the stamp step and the awk program |
| Lane A grows from six units to twelve in one serial lane | Warning | Run `u1-fork-release-hardening` and `u1-fork-attestations` as their own worktree lane (Execution order) |
| `u1-lane-issues-freshness` grows to L with the stop, the published basis and three workflow items | Warning | Each part has its own tests; the session may split the three workflow items into a unit of their own without moving any criterion |
| A consumer that tests `$? -eq 1` for drift sees 3 | Minor | CHANGELOG `### Changed`; `docs/cli-reference.md` lists four statuses; every non-zero check stays a failure |
| A committed ledger hash forged to match an edited engine file reads `resync` | Minor | Exit 3 is never a pass and its remedy rewrites the file from the engine; the guide says never merge on 3 without the pinned sync; file 0's ledger bound limits which paths a row may name |
| Walking up from a subfolder makes `clean -y` there remove the root's setup | Warning | The stderr line names the root before anything runs; the interactive confirmation names the root's count; `clean -y` keeps user state (REQ-FLOW-028) |
| `.gitignore` comment lines are no longer screened, so an injection phrase there is kept | Minor | No client loads `.gitignore` into a prompt; agents reading it see owner text, as with any repository file the engine does not republish into agent context |
| `.stamity/backups/` grows without bound | Minor | Local and ignored; `clean -y --purge` removes it; follow-up row for a prune |
| Unwiring Copilot's guard drops its telemetry row | Minor | It never refused anything (identity-free payload); the capability matrix says so |
| The verbatim prepend leaves an odd-looking gap for an owner file that starts with blank lines | Minor | Cosmetic; it is the owner's bytes, and the strip returns them exactly |
| Lane B grows from nine units to thirteen (one moved to file 0, five added) and shares `check.ts`, `init/apply.ts`, `sync/engine.ts` and `init.ts` | Warning | One writer at a time in the order below; the orchestrator may split lane B into two sessions at `u1-copilot-setup-steps` |
| File 0 contracts move under these units (the `coOwned` schema, the importChoice proof, `check --expect-*`, per-entry hook-file ownership) | Warning | Each unit names `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-00.md` where it reads one; intake re-reads file 0 as landed (the freshness guard) |
| The doctor-row count words drift as file 0, file 01 and file 02 add rows | Minor | Each unit re-counts at intake; `test/docsPages.test.ts:2755-2785` holds the troubleshooting table set-equal with the probes |
| `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` in foreign clients stays unmeasured if the accounts are missing at execution | Minor | The emitted tail is safe for both answers; the docs table says "unmeasured" with the date |

## Open questions

None. The spec author's two markers in this slice are settled above (S8, S9), and the drafters' corrections — the
plugins preset's optional build group, the companion preset's npm versioning, `--suffix`'s exit code, the fixture's
behind count, the folder rule for foreign paths, the MCP documents' proof by re-rendering and the two content-equal
residues — are folded into the spec delta.

**Amended 2026-10-06.** None open. The drafters' readings are settled as declared defaults the maintainer may reverse before a session starts: the lane's re-lock is an offline root rewrite checked by `npm ci --ignore-scripts` (S13); the App variable holds the App's client id (S17); the default worker cap is 4 (S28); `check` exits 3 only when every drift row is a re-sync and a pending deletion always reads as tampering (S35); a deliberate nested setup takes `init --force` (S37); `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` stamps `init` and `sync` alike (S39); `config set importChoice.<path>` offers `replace` and moves the owner's bytes itself (S33); the managed Copilot setup steps read no project workflow (S8, revised). Measured at execution, not guessed: whether Cursor and the Copilot CLI set `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` when they run imported Claude hooks (`u1-node-missing-hint`; the emitted tail is safe either way), whether the per-run token reads package visibility (a warning arm until then), and whether "Allow GitHub Actions to create and approve pull requests" also governs App tokens.

## Follow-ups (appended to `.stamity/inbox.md` at this plan's write)

- Minor · test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts:282 · the release-manifest case pins `plugin-dist` and `plugins/v<version>` on the checkout's own build, so a fork that sets its own `stamity.distribution.branch` or `tagPattern` fails its inherited gate; derive both from the resolved identity.
- Minor · scripts/fork-identity.mjs · the Renovate presets assume the default `plugins/v<version>` tag pattern; a fork with its own `tagPattern` gets a tag manager that matches nothing; retarget `matchStrings` and the versioning prefix from the resolved pattern.
- Minor · test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts:325-389, test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts:554, test/upstream/lane.test.ts:253,1866,1882, test/ci/pluginLifecycle.test.ts:523,665 · https-userinfo and token-assignment placeholders (`fixture-user:fixture-pass@`, `x-access-token:SECRET@`, `fixture-token-…`) are not provider-format secrets and stay; a scanner with a generic URI-credential or assignment detector may still flag them; build them at run time if a fork reports one.
- Minor · docs/plugins.md:534-545 · the Renovate presets passage does not say that a fork releasing suffixed versions runs `fork-identity.mjs --suffix`.
- Minor · .github/workflows/release.yml · the canonical rehearsal (`dry-run-summary`) runs no `npm publish --dry-run`; harmless while canonical versions are never prereleases, but a release-candidate version would meet npm 11's tag rule only at publish.
- Minor · src/cli/notice/updateNotice.ts:270 · the notice reads `latest` only, so a fork that sets `STAMITY_RELEASE_DIST_TAG` gets no notice for its channel; read the channel, or keep the documented limit.
- Minor · src/merge/safeWrite.ts:602-606 · `prependManagedBlock`'s `trimStart` and final-newline normalisation keep a supplemented file's round trip inexact when the original starts with whitespace or lacks a final newline; record the original's shape or stop normalising.
- Minor · src/adapters/cursor.ts:811-816 · Cursor's `failClosed` guards (and the Copilot and Codex hooks) cannot print a Node hint: measure each client's hook shell on Windows, then add a launch tail where it parses.
- Minor · src/shared/cliCall.ts:4-23 · the version stays in ~20 generated files as pinned CLI calls; a version-less launcher that reads the pin from the manifest would leave it only in the manifest (see Concerns).
- Minor · src/cli/commands/check.ts:763-802 · repositories already doubled by an earlier `init --force` keep the duplicate; `preserved-duplicate` warns, and no heal path exists.

- Minor · src/adapters/codex.ts · `.codex/config.toml` is a whole-file engine output; a key another tool adds there is drift (`sync` `.bak`, `clean` skip) — co-own it by key if a tool starts writing it.
- Minor · src/cli/commands/config/mcp.ts:463-469 · `config mcp remove` leaves the `.env.mcp` line until the next `sync`.
